### [1] [A simple counterexample to the Hohenberg-Kohn theorem for excited states](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28296)
*Dominic Shillingford*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文构造了一个一维环面上非相互作用费米子系统在激发态下的Hohenberg-Kohn定理反例：对偶数个粒子，存在一族平移等价但不同的外势，对应相同的首激发态单粒子密度；奇数情形在非共振谱假设下亦成立。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检验Hohenberg-Kohn定理是否可推广至激发态——即激发态单粒子密度能否唯一确定外势。

Method: 利用可积Lamé势的精确解，构造具有常数首激发态密度的n体非相互作用费米子系统；通过势函数平移生成非平凡势族；结合谱非共振条件延拓至奇数粒子数情形。

Result: 证明对任意偶数n=2l，存在外势族{v_*(x−a)}_{a∈𝕋}，其首激发态密度恒为常数，故势→密度映射非单射；奇数n≥3时，在谱非共振条件下同样得到激发态密度非唯一性。

Conclusion: Hohenberg-Kohn型唯一性在激发态不成立，即使在最简单的非相互作用、一维周期体系中亦存在本质反例；该现象与平面弹性曲线分类（n=2时）存在几何联系。

Abstract: For an even number of non-interacting fermions on the one-dimensional unit torus, a semi-explicit counterexample to the Hohenberg--Kohn theorem for excited states is exhibited. Indeed, for each $n = 2l$, it is shown that there exist constants $m \in (0, 1)$ and $\ell > 0$ such that an $n$-body system with the rescaled Lamé potential $v_*(x) = 2m \ell^2 \operatorname{sn}^2(\ell x; m)$ has a simple first excited energy and a first excited-state particle density which is constant on the torus. By translating this potential, we exhibit a non-trivial one-parameter family of potentials, all of which have a constant first excited-state density. As such, the potential-to-density map is not one-to-one in this setting. Under a spectral nonresonance assumption, we obtain the same nonuniqueness at a simple excited energy for odd $n \geq 3$. We also explore connections to the classification problem of elastica in the plane when $n = 2$, and outline some related questions of interest.

</details>


### [2] [Effective Hamiltonians and asymptotic band coupling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28328)
*Guillaume Bal,Paul Cazeaux,Daniel Massatt,Solomon Quinn*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文通过多尺度渐近展开方法，推导出具有慢变空间调制的一类紧束缚薛定谔方程的有效宏观模型；适用于谱局域于色散关系中（可能简并）点(K,E)的波包，模型在任意阶渐近精度下给出长时间动力学逼近。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 发展适用于谱局域（含能带简并）波包的高精度、长时有效的宏观演化模型，以刻画慢变调制下紧束缚系统的非绝热耦合效应。

Method: 多尺度渐近展开法（multiple-scale asymptotic expansion），在简并子空间上构造有效哈密顿量，显式量化简并能带与非简并能带间的耦合。

Result: 获得任意阶渐近精度的有效宏观模型；该模型适用于一般简并情形（不要求全简并），且能精确描述能带间非绝热跃迁与耦合动力学。

Conclusion: 所建立的框架统一处理了从非简并到部分简并的谱局域波包演化问题，为强关联离散系统（如拓扑晶格、冷原子光晶格）中的波包操控与输运建模提供了严格数学基础。

Abstract: By a multiple-scale asymptotic expansion, we derive effective macroscopic models for a large class of tight-binding Schrödinger equations with slow spatial variations. For wave packets that are spectrally localized to some point $(K;E) \in \mathbb{R}^d \times \mathbb{R}$ in the unperturbed tight-binding dispersion relation, our effective models produce long-time approximations of the tight-binding dynamics, accurate to any prescribed order in the asymptotic parameter. Our framework allows the point $(K;E)$ to be degenerate, meaning that multiple (but not necessarily all) energy bands of the tight-binding Hamiltonian can attain the value $E$ at the frequency $K$. We construct an effective Hamiltonian on the relevant degenerate subspace, which quantifies the coupling between the degenerate and other bands. Several applications and numerical simulations illustrate the theory.

</details>


### [3] [On a class of affine Temperley-Lieb modules](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28389)
*Yacine Ikhlef*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文证明了仿射Temperley-Lieb模类在图示张量积下构成拟张量（即幺半、辫）范畴；当q非单位根时，其由可约标准模的单商生成的无穷子范畴是半单ribbon范畴，并给出证据表明该子范畴中每个单模等价于一对U_q(sl_2)最高权模。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 建立仿射Temperley-Lieb代数表示范畴的幺半与辫结构，以连接其融合范畴与量子群U_q(sl_2)的表示论，为共形场论和拓扑序提供代数基础。

Method: 运用图示张量积构造，结合标准模理论、商模分类、辫幺半范畴公理验证及融合规则与结合同构的显式分析。

Result: 1) 全体仿射TL模在图示张量积下构成quasitensor（即braided monoidal）范畴；2) q非单位根时，由标准模单商生成的子范畴是半单ribbon范畴；3) 每个此类单模在融合范畴意义下等价于一对U_q(sl_2)最高权模。

Conclusion: 仿射Temperley-Lieb范畴在非根情形下展现出与U_q(sl_2)表示范畴深层的融合等价性，支持其作为量子群对称性在临界统计模型与二维CFT中的代数实现。

Abstract: We prove that the class of all affine Temperley-Lieb modules, equipped with the recently introduced "diagrammatic" tensor product, forms a quasitensor (i.e. monoidal braided) category. When the Temperley-Lieb parameter $q$ is not a root of unity, we consider the infinite subcategory generated by the simple quotients of reducible standard modules, and we argue that it is a semi-simple ribbon category. We present some evidence that, under the fusion rules and the associativity maps, each simple module in this subcategory is equivalent to a pair of $U_q(sl_2)$ heighest-weight modules.

</details>


### [4] [Integrable Orbital Exchange between Ellipsoidally Deformed $p$, $d$, and $f$ Electron States: Two-Angle Sklyanin Representations and Fused Elliptic $R$-Matrices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28444)

Main category: math-ph

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 构建具有几何畸变（椭球形）背景下的可积多体轨道交换模型，以统一描述强关联体系中角动量自由度与形状自由度（畸变）的耦合动力学，并拓展已知椭圆可积模型（如Fateev、Takhtajan–Babujian）至更一般的轨道对称性框架。

Method: 采用Sklyanin代数的两参数（双角）实现，构造Jacobi型差分算子D和L_i；通过椭球坐标系下的confocal ellipsoid几何映射，将轨道波函数定义在椭球面上，并利用Sklyanin积分构造正定内积；借助椭圆融合程序（elliptic fusion）导出角动量生成元上的显式R矩阵，并验证其满足Yang–Baxter方程；由此生成交换哈密顿量族及其对易守恒量。

Result: 获得显式Jacobi差分算子D、L_i及其完整本征谱与正交归一本征函数；建立p-sector中坐标函数承载精确自旋-1表示，且D作用实现椭球→球面的保结构映射；导出角动量空间上的椭圆R矩阵，满足标准Yang–Baxter关系；构造出均匀链、交替链及同中心混合对（mixed-pair）交换哈密顿量；证明所有交换作用保持每个局域成分的两个Casimir算子：C_1 = J^2（角动量平方）与C_2 = T_{dist+rot}（畸变-转动动能型不变量）；p–p模型严格复现Fateev模型，并在η→0极限下还原为Takhtajan–Babujian自旋-1链。

Conclusion: 椭球形轨道多重态提供了自然的几何可积平台，使得轨道交换相互作用可在保持局域角动量与畸变-转动动能双重守恒的前提下实现椭圆可积性；该框架不仅统一了若干经典自旋链模型，还为研究强关联体系中轨道自由度与晶格畸变协同效应提供了严格可解范例。

Abstract: We construct integrable orbital exchange models from ellipsoidally deformed $p$, $d$, and $f$ multiplets. A two-angle realization of the Sklyanin algebra gives explicit Jacobi difference operators $D,L_i$, complete distortion spectra and eigenfunctions, and a positive inner product transported from Sklyanin's integral. In the $p$ sector the coordinate functions carry an exact spin-one action, and applying $D$ componentwise maps the confocal ellipsoid to a sphere. Elliptic fusion gives explicit $R$-matrices in the angular generators on both factors. Their Yang--Baxter relations yield commuting charges and exchange Hamiltonians for homogeneous and alternating chains, as well as mixed-pair interactions that can act between angular degrees of freedom on the same center. Exchange changes angular projections and correlations while preserving the two local Casimirs of each constituent, which fix its angular-momentum-squared and distortion-plus-rotation kinetic-energy invariants. The $p$--$p$ model recovers Fateev's elliptic spin-one interaction and reduces to the Takhtajan--Babujian Hamiltonian in the rational isotropic limit.

</details>


### [5] [An Elasticity Complex for Kirchhoff--Love Shells](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28480)
*Hang Yin,Albert Chern*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文构建了基于线性化Gauss-Codazzi方程的Kirchhoff-Love壳理论弹性复形，揭示其作为无穷小刚体运动层的细分解，并导出应力场的Airy势表示、自然边界条件，以及带边界的壳上应变与应力场的Hodge-Morrey-Friedrichs分解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为建立壳体理论中几何相容性、力学平衡与边界行为的统一微分同调框架，需将经典弹性复形推广至曲面嵌入情形，并连接层上同调、势理论与边界值问题。

Method: 采用微分几何与同调代数方法：以线性化Gauss-Codazzi方程为应变相容性条件构造弹性复形；利用层论识别其为无穷小刚体运动层的细分解；通过复形正合性导出Airy势存在性；结合Hodge理论在带边黎曼流形（壳面）上建立Morrey-Friedrichs型正交分解。

Result: 1) 构造了Kirchhoff-Love壳的弹性复形，其零阶上同调同构于壳面的无穷小刚体运动空间；2) 证明平衡应力场局部可表为Airy应力势的协变Laplacian；3) 导出位移与力边界条件的微分形式自然配对；4) 建立应变与应力场在L²-范数下的Hodge-Morrey-Friedrichs正交分解。

Conclusion: 该复形将壳体弹性理论提升至微分同调层面，统一刻画几何相容性、静力平衡、边界条件与场分解，为数值离散（如有限元外微分法）和奇异摄动分析提供严格数学基础。

Abstract: We develop an elasticity complex for Kirchhoff--Love shell theory, based on the linearized Gauss--Codazzi equations as compatibility conditions for shell strains. The resulting shell complex forms a fine resolution of the sheaf of infinitesimal rigid motions, so its cohomology groups are determined by the corresponding sheaf cohomology. Remarkably, the complex also reveals an Airy potential representation of equilibrium stress fields. The framework further identifies natural boundary conditions for both kinematic and dynamic variables. Finally, we establish a Hodge--Morrey--Friedrichs decomposition for strain and stress fields on shells with boundary.

</details>


### [6] [Feshbach resonances of the second order in a Friedrichs model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28743)
*Dmitry S. Grudko,Alexander K. Motovilov*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了矩阵Friedrichs模型的扩展版本，该模型可产生二阶Feshbach共振；随着耦合常数增大，嵌入连续谱中的未扰动哈密顿量本征值对首先演化为一阶共振，随后合并为单个二阶共振，并给出了这一现象发生的条件；基于共振的算子解释，发现转移函数的算子根具有特殊结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解高能物理中Feshbach共振的高阶行为，特别是二阶共振的产生机制及其数学刻画。

Method: 采用算子理论方法，分析扩展矩阵Friedrichs模型中转移函数的算子根结构，并研究其随耦合常数变化的谱演化行为。

Result: 确定了两个一阶共振合并为单个二阶共振的临界条件，并揭示了对应转移函数算子根的特殊代数结构（如Jordan块或广义本征向量结构）。

Conclusion: 二阶Feshbach共振对应于转移函数在复能量平面上的二阶极点，其出现要求未扰动谱点具有特定简并性与耦合对称性，且算子根呈现非对角Jordan型结构。

Abstract: We consider an extended version of the matrix Friedrichs model that may produce Feshbach resonances of the second order. In this model, under enlarging of the coupling constant, a pair of eigenvalues of the unperturbed Hamiltonian embedded into the continuous spectrum first transforms in resonances of the first order. At some moment later on, these two resonances merge into a single resonance but of the second order, and we fix conditions for this event. Being based on the operator interpretation of resonances, we found that, in the case under consideration, the operator roots of the transfer function have a very particular structure.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 发展一种既满足量子统计（泡利原理）又适用于双极输运（含带间散射）的高效微观模拟方法，以克服传统蒙特卡洛方法在石墨烯中因价带高度占据导致的计算低效问题。

Method: 基于价带空位表象（valence-vacancy representation）构建Pauli-consistent ensemble Monte Carlo框架；显式建模声子辅助的带间散射（含e-h generation/recombination）；在自由飞行与所有散射事件中强制执行单粒子态占据数0/1约束，并严格守恒净电荷；通过敏感性分析、网格细化及与间断伽辽金（discontinuous Galerkin）确定性求解器对比验证数值可靠性。

Result: 该方法在时间演化、载流子分布函数及稳态输运观测量（如电流、载流子密度）上与独立的确定性DG解高度一致；在弹道、扩散及过渡输运区均保持精度；证实其具备实际微观模拟双极石墨烯输运的可行性与效率。

Abstract: We develop a Pauli-consistent ensemble Monte Carlo method for bipolar charge transport in graphene, with an explicit treatment of phonon-assisted interband scattering, including electron-hole generation and recombination. The method employs a valence-vacancy representation to describe coupled electron and hole populations without explicitly simulating the highly populated valence band. It preserves the discrete occupation bounds during both free flight and scattering while conserving net charge. The numerical properties of the method are assessed through sensitivity and refinement studies and comparison with an independent deterministic discontinuous Galerkin solution of the same kinetic model. The two approaches show very good agreement for the time evolution, carrier distributions, and steady-state transport observables across the investigated transport regimes. The results also demonstrate the computational practicality of the method for microscopic simulations of bipolar graphene transport.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>

Conclusion: theta函数参数化为具有三角对称性的球面物理问题（如Jahn–Teller耦合、多极序参量、非线性晶格动力学）提供了兼具代数结构与微分几何兼容性的新坐标框架，揭示了Volterra链、椭圆模函数与球谐表示之间的深层联系。

Abstract: We construct a theta-function parametrization of the unit sphere adapted to the eight trigonal directions. The construction follows from a cubic theta identity discussed by Bobek and admits an equivalent representation in Jacobi elliptic functions. The angular parameter and theta nome give a nonorthogonal coordinate system whose squared Cartesian components are related to the three-component Volterra chain. An algebraic change of angle, retaining the nome, yields orthogonal coordinates with explicit metric coefficients and a diagonal Laplace--Beltrami operator. We apply both parametrizations to the geometry of O'Brien's reduced Jahn--Teller model and give compact expressions for real spherical harmonics of degrees two and three. The orthogonal angle is the polar angle of an $E_g$ quadrupolar doublet, while one octupolar harmonic depends only on the nome within each octant.

</details>


### [9] [Spin-geodesic systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29005)
*Renato Feres,Zijie Hu*

TL;DR: 本文引入并研究了一类定义在黎曼流形上的几何动力系统——自旋测地系统，其中质点运动通过一个对称端omorphism场B与内禀经典自旋耦合；该系统可视为测地流的B-依赖形变，并等价于某抽象环境几何中的无滑滚动系统；进一步证明了其相空间上保持标准Liouville体积（Liouville定理），尽管该流仅在B≡0时为辛流。

Abstract: This paper concerns transport along an interface between two insulators modeled by self-adjoint differential systems $H$ of arbitrary order, coupled to a magnetic field either by Peierls substitution or, for operators of the form $L^*L+W$, in the factor $L$. The principal symbol of $H$ is either elliptic or has a kernel of constant rank, in which case finitely many bands remain bounded at large momentum, as in rotating shallow water, cold plasmas, Maxwell's equations and pseudospin-one materials. Ellipticity is then replaced by a Douglis-Nirenberg structure and by a gap condition on an effective flat symbol. We show that, for every energy $\alpha$ away from the flat-band set and in a spectral gap of both bulks, the interface current $\sigma_I=\mathrm{Tr}\,i[H,P]\varphi'(H)$ is well defined, with $2\pi\sigma_I$ a Fredholm index, and that it is given, for all walls in the elliptic case and under a freezing condition otherwise, by a bulk-interface correspondence of the form $2\pi\sigma_I=J(a_+,B_+;\alpha)-J(a_-,B_-;\alpha)+\mathsf{C}$. For a nonzero bulk field, $J=2\pi\sigma_H$ is the Kubo-Chern Hall conductivity, which we compute by an extension-corrected count of generalized Landau levels. For a vanishing field, $J$ is the Chern integral of the field-free symbol, which is also, under a weak-field condition, the weak-field limit of the Hall conductivity. The term $\mathsf{C}$ is a Chern integral of the occupied projector at infinite momentum across the wall. The current is stable under large perturbations, and boundaries of the applied field inside a homogeneous material carry quantized currents that change at field-induced transitions. Near flat-band energies, where generalized Landau levels may accumulate, the current may diverge.

In this proceedings article, we analyze the structure of the combined theory and the different strategies to simulate it on the lattice, as well as presenting novel results of lattice QCD+QED simulations at finite temperature, focusing on understanding the role of the electromagnetic coupling in the thermodynamics of this system. We use Hamiltonian eigenvectors to analyze the model-basis compositions. A contamination function then compares the resulting operator-mixing pattern with lattice correlation-matrix eigenvectors. Within the scenarios considered, both bare states and coupled-channel interactions play important roles in describing the two resonances. From our combined infinite and finite volume analysis, in contrast to conventional quark-model expectations, the spin-$1/2$ bare state is heavier than the spin-$3/2$ bare state. The contamination analysis is consistent with the associated operator assignment and with a recent analysis of the relativistic wavefunctions associated with these states. The symmetry-constrained NPHF provides a unified description of the two $S_{11}$ resonances. The combined analysis also constrains their model interpretation.

Abstract: The search for Higgs bosons Beyond the Standard Model (BSM) began intensively at the Large Electron-Positron (LEP) collider (1989-2000), continued at the Tevatron (2001-2011), and entered a new era with the start of the Large Hadron Collider (LHC) in 2010. While the discovery of a Higgs boson in 2012 established the Standard Model mechanism of electroweak symmetry breaking, it also opened a wide landscape of open questions that motivate ongoing BSM Higgs searches. This review traces the evolution of these searches across experiments, highlighting both experimental techniques and underlying theoretical ideas in an accessible manner. Emphasis is placed on how different collider environments shaped search strategies and how seemingly subtle experimental effects, such as small excesses, non-standard decay topologies, or suppressed couplings, can provide insight into the possible structure of extended Higgs sectors. By connecting historical developments with current analyses and future prospects, this article aims not only to summarise results but also to provide students and newcomers with intuitive insight into how Higgs searches are designed, interpreted, and continuously refined. It illustrates how experimental challenges translate into physics opportunities, thereby inviting the next generation of researchers to contribute to one of the central questions in particle physics: whether the Higgs sector is minimal or part of a more complex structure.

Abstract: The long-standing discrepancy between beam and bottle measurements of the free-neutron lifetime---known as the neutron lifetime puzzle---remains an open anomaly in nuclear and particle physics. Here, we investigate whether collective quantum synchronization via macroscopic Dicke superradiance can influence free-neutron decay or mediate the relaxation of hypothetical excited neutron states. We show that superradiance is fundamentally obstructed in standard three-body weak beta decay by continuous final-state phase space and fermionic antisymmetrization, and remains inactive in terrestrial ultracold neutron traps due to low particle densities, even within the Koch--Hummel excited-neutron model. Extending this analysis to the primordial plasma prior to Big Bang nucleosynthesis, we demonstrate that rapid $e^\pm$ collisional dephasing and intense thermal photon backgrounds prevent a superradiant purge and instead enforce thermal detailed balance. Abstract: We compute the cross section for coherent diffractive $D^0$ photoproduction in ultraperipheral lead-lead and proton-lead collisions (UPCs) at the Large Hadron Collider (LHC) within the Color Glass Condensate (CGC) effective field theory. Abstract: In this work, we investigate helicity-dependent fermion production driven by a pseudoscalar Yukawa interaction within the one-loop effective action framework. For an undamped inflaton background, the temporal symmetry around successive zero crossings leads to the cancellation of helicity-mixing contributions after an even number of events, preventing the accumulation of helicity asymmetry. We then extend the analysis to a damped oscillatory background, in which the decreasing inflaton amplitude breaks this temporal symmetry and modifies the transfer matrix at each crossing. The resulting non-commuting transfer matrices produce a persistent helical spectrum, as demonstrated by the probabilities as a function of momentum obtained after successive scattering events. A comparison with scalar Yukawa preheating further confirms the role of helicity mixing in generating the helical asymmetry induced by pseudoscalar mechanism. These results establish an effective action framework for studying helicity asymmetry during fermionic preheating and provide insight into the influence of the inflaton's dynamical evolution on particle production.

Abstract: In this study, a quasi-exactly solvable sextic potential is analyzed by two diagrammatic calculations. Using only Feynman diagrams, we verify this cancellation explicitly through third order. After continuation to negative coupling, the formal zero mode becomes nonnormalizable. A real instanton remains, but its one-instanton contribution to \(E_0\) vanishes. In particular, the leading shift in energy arises from a complex neutral bion. Taking an exact constrained family, we calculate its local median amplitude through two loops. The direct diagrammatic sum gives $c_{\rm 2L}^{\rm diag}=-0.750000(5)$, in excellent agreement with the exact-WKB value $-3/4$.

Abstract: Quantum magic is a resource for universal quantum computation and may also provide a probe of fundamental interactions. Abstract: A conventional narrative developed for high energy heavy ion collisions prior to the RHIC program assumed hadron production dominated by hot and dense QGP formation, expansion and freezeout. A similar program has been applied to jet-related contributions to spectra and angular correlations with a comparable result, a quadratic trend on $n_{ch}$ as expected for two-gluon interactions. In $A$ and $J$, a local gluon-condensate term that does not fall with $Q^2$ is the largest, and their gluon shares are well below the lattice, possibly because the gluon EMT couples at this order only through gluon exchange and the condensate. Abstract: We investigate the effects of fermionic dark matter (DM) and scalar-cut potential ($U_{\text{cut}}(\phi)$) on the equation of state (EOS) and macroscopic properties of neutron stars. The baryonic matter consists of neutrons, protons, electrons, and muons under the $\beta$-equilibrium and is described within the chiral SU(3) quark mean-field (CQMF) model. In this framework, quarks are confined within baryons, whose properties are modified through the exchange of scalar fields $\sigma$, $\zeta$, and $\delta$, and vector fields $\omega$ and $\rho$. The DM is modelled as a self-interacting fermionic fluid with scalar $\varphi_D$ and vector $V_D^\mu$ mediators. The baryonic and DM fluids interact only through gravity and are treated within a two-fluid Tolman-Oppenheimer-Volkoff formalism. We examine the resulting mass-radius relation and tidal deformability, along with the gravitational redshift, to investigate the effects of DM on neutron star structure and observable properties. The inclusion of $U_{\text{cut}}(\phi)$ stiffens the EOS, whereas increasing the DM fraction generally softens the EOS and modifies the mass-radius relation, tidal deformability, and gravitational redshift. The magnitude and nature of these modifications depend on the DM particle mass and mediator interactions, which determine whether the resulting configurations exhibit compact DM cores or extended DM halos.

We find that it becomes comparable to the latter for $m_\pi L\lesssim 1.5$--$1.7$ at rest, and that it is larger in moving frames, reaching $m_\pi L\lesssim 2.8$ for $\vec d=[002]$. Abstract: Classicalization is a non-Wilsonian framework in which an effective field theory self-completes above its strong-coupling scale by generating semiclassical objects that decay into many soft quanta, thereby realizing ultraviolet-infrared mixing. Abstract: We propose a minimal fermionic realisation of the $\textit{dark}$ linear seesaw with spontaneous breaking of lepton number. The small lepton-number-violating parameter responsible for linear-seesaw neutrino masses is generated radiatively by a $\textit{dark}$ sector consisting of a singlet and a doublet fermion, together with scalar singlets. This breaking yields a Majoron $J$ and makes the lightest $\textit{dark}$ particle stable. The charged $\textit{dark}$ fermion acquires its mass solely from U(1)$_L$ breaking and thus induces an anomalous Majoron coupling to photons. Moreover, both sectors enhance the Majoron couplings to charged leptons, so that the Majoron-emitting decays $\tau \to \ell J$ ($\ell = e,\mu$) typically exceed $\tau \to \ell\gamma$ by orders of magnitude and can probe parameter space beyond the reach of $\mu \to e\gamma$.

Abstract: Self-supervised learning (SSL) and explicit physical inductive biases have driven major advances in jet tagging, yet they are typically pursued along separate tracks. We demonstrate that enforcing Lorentz symmetry in the latent space is critical for representation quality and that this can be achieved either architecturally or learned via kinematic augmentations. With a standard non-invariant Transformer backbone, LeJEPA outperforms established autoregressive, masked particle modelling, and self-distillation baselines in linear- and MLP-probe accuracy, with L-GATr-slim providing further complementary gains. Conclusion: B−L/3守恒型TeV尺度BNV尚未被Super-K排除，LHC可通过专用末态（\bar{t}ℓ^+ℓ^+\cancel{E}_T）开展互补探测；所设计筛选策略具模型普适性，适用于更广BNV有效算符对应的LHC信号。

Abstract: We investigate the compatibility of existing limits on trilepton nucleon decay by the Super-Kamiokande (Super-K) experiment with Baryon Number Violation (BNV) at the TeV scale and motivate a dedicated search using existing data at the LHC. Working within an effective field theory framework, we derive model-independent constraints on higher-dimensional operators associated with $B - L/3$ conserving texture of BNV. We demonstrate that existing Super-K limits on loop-level trilepton nucleon decay provide significantly stronger constraints compared to tree-level charm-baryon decays induced by these operators. These constraints translate into bounds of $O(1-30)$ TeV on the BNV scale, leaving open the possibility of an LHC search for $B - L/3$ type BNV. Motivated by this complementarity, we investigate the collider phenomenology of a representative UV completion featuring an $s$-channel diquark resonance that decays into a leptoquark--dilepton pair, producing a distinctive final state containing an anti-top quark, two charged anti-leptons, and missing transverse energy. Our results indicate that, although same-sign dilepton searches by ATLAS and CMS already place indirect constraints on this model, a dedicated analysis based on the selection cuts we develop significantly extends the reach for our chosen benchmarks. In particular, using only Run 2 data, we demonstrate complementary reach to limits on trilepton nucleon decay searches set by the Super-K experiment and to the projected reach of Hyper-Kamiokande. The selection cuts are applicable beyond our representative model.

</details>

Conclusion: 新约束与传统约束高度互补，显著提升了bootstrap方法对含多个质量部分子的三圈软反常维数的预测能力，为更一般情形的高阶计算铺平道路。

Abstract: The soft anomalous dimension governs the universal long-distance singularities of gauge theory amplitudes. It exhibits a highly constrained analytic structure and has been shown to be amenable to bootstrap methods. In this paper we revisit the relation between unitarity cuts and sequential discontinuities of Feynman integrals and use it to derive new constraints on the soft anomalous dimension. At three loops, we show that certain sequential discontinuities in non-overlapping two-particle channels vanish for every web contributing to the quadrupole function that correlates four coloured partons. These constraints hold for arbitrary parton masses, and we explicitly verify them for the known massless and one-mass cases. We then demonstrate their constraining power by substantially strengthening the massless bootstrap and carrying out the first bootstrap construction of the one-mass quadrupole function. The new constraints are largely complementary to those arising from the absence of unphysical branch points, symmetries, and collinear and Regge limits. With their help, we determine each quadrupole function within the assumed function space up to a single coefficient, which we fix by matching to existing diagrammatic results. These results open the way to bootstrapping the three-loop soft anomalous dimension for amplitudes with several massive partons.

</details>

Abstract: We prove that the renormalisation group flow of multiscalar $\phi^4$ theory in four dimensions is a gradient flow to all orders in perturbation theory. The proof uses the Weyl consistency conditions of Osborn and Jack, and it assumes two properties of perturbation theory: that the generating functional with local couplings is finite (also assumed by Osborn and Jack), and that the known expansion of massive Feynman integrals at large momenta holds with a bound on its remainder at each momentum. The consistency conditions give a gradient formula in which the tensor that multiplies the beta function is allowed to have an antisymmetric part, determined by coefficients of the Weyl anomaly. We derive a Ward identity for the two-point function of the marginal operators at constant couplings away from fixed points, with sources for the currents of field rotations and for the mass operator. It re-expresses one of the anti-symmetric contributions in terms of two-point functions and the first moment of the three-point function. We show that, after this rewrite, each of the resulting terms is equivalent to a symmetric contribution to the metric and a contribution to the potential, and we give both explicitly. The metric is positive definite to all orders in perturbation theory. The proof implies in particular the four relations among five- and six-loop coefficients of the field rotation, which Pannell and Stergiou derived as the conditions for a gradient flow at six loops. We check by explicit computation that these relations indeed hold. A companion paper treats $d=4-\varepsilon$ and other critical dimensions with a Ward identity on the sphere.

Abstract: We show that Rubakov-Callan catalysis of proton decay in the field of a 't Hooft-Polyakov monopole proceeds through "sourced SU(2) instantons", which are novel topological gauge configurations sourced by fermions and carrying unit instanton number. We relate the winding number to the number of fermionic zero modes, suggesting an underlying index theorem for sourced instantons. We state properties of the renormalised functional with local couplings on the sphere $S^d$: finiteness, a local Weyl anomaly, the Ward identity of a conformal Killing vector, rotation properties and statements on the free theory. From them we derive a Ward identity without anomaly for a combination of the functional and its anomaly. Its first variation is a gradient formula whose symmetric metric contains the two-point function on the sphere and whose potential at fixed points is, in an extended form of the properties, proportional to the sphere free energy $\tilde F$ up to a constant. Sources of relevant operators enter through their Weyl variation, and in a scheme which we construct the gradient formula persists, with a nondegenerate metric if a matrix of the free theory is invertible. For multiscalar $\phi^4$ theory and for scalars and Dirac fermions with Yukawa and quartic couplings in $d=4-\varepsilon$, for sextic scalars in $d=3-\varepsilon$ and for cubic scalars in $d=6-\varepsilon$, we derive all these properties from a finiteness hypothesis. The leading coefficient factorizes into a hard part and a soft vertex. Late-time estimates treat the hard part exactly but approximate the soft vertex by its late-time limit, whereas we compute both exactly. Without a chemical potential, the exact coefficient differs from the late-time one by a universal factor that depends only on the masses in the loop and the coupling at the soft vertex. For the heavy Standard Model loops, the exact signals are ten to thirty times larger than their late-time estimates. With a chemical potential, the ratio depends on the model, and at our benchmarks it reaches a factor of thirty for helical vectors and about a hundred for the fermion triangle, where we agree with recent exact results. Away from the squeezed limit the leading term soon fails, mainly through a drift in the phase of the oscillation, which we derive for scalar and fermion loops.

The spacetime metric and equation of state determine a Lagrangian submanifold, whose intersection with the coisotropic subvariety is precisely the Lichnerowicz--Carter system. Abstract: We revisit non-relativistic quantum-mechanical scattering in a Coulomb potential. The long-range nature of the interaction leads to divergent integrals in conventional S-matrix calculations using the Born series. Dollard showed that a well-defined scattering operator can be constructed by replacing the free Hamiltonian $ H_0 $ with an appropriate asymptotic Hamiltonian $ H_{\rm D} $. However, explicit perturbative calculations within Dollard's framework have remained scarce. We develop a perturbative formulation of the Dollard scattering operator in which the Coulomb divergences cancel to all orders. Perturbatively, we demonstrate this cancellation explicitly through second Born order and show that the resulting amplitude reproduces the known exact distributional Coulomb amplitude, including its forward-scattering contribution. With the aim of generalizing our analysis to quantum field theory, we reformulate the problem in the Heisenberg picture. We construct a new set of canonical variables through a time-dependent canonical transformation that removes the logarithmic Coulomb correction from the asymptotic phase-space coordinates. Using these variables, we construct the corresponding Heisenberg S-matrix through several complementary approaches. This leads to a formulation of the Lehmann--Symanzik--Zimmermann (LSZ) reduction formalism adapted to the modified asymptotic dynamics, which we relate to the Dollard scattering operator. This construction provides a framework for extending the LSZ formalism to infrared divergences in relativistic quantum field theories with long-range interactions and massless fields.

Conclusion: 不同环境模型（弦云、Kiselev、对数型）导致显著差异的视界结构与测试粒子动力学行为，其有效应力张量各向异性排除了齐次精质或压力为零冷暗物质的识别；所得结果严格限定于几何视界边界与局域测试粒子运动学，不蕴含双黑洞相互作用力律、宇宙加速机制，亦不构成弱引力猜想的验证。

Abstract: We study horizon mergers and the initial radial acceleration of charged test particles in static, spherically symmetric black-hole geometries with a string cloud, a Kiselev power-law component, or a logarithmic profile conventionally. After separating the Maxwell contribution, the effective environmental stress tensors are anisotropic and are not identified with homogeneous quintessence or pressureless cold dark matter. Using a common mass-function formulation, we derive the horizon-merger conditions and classify the Kiselev branches. In this family, the inner-event merger charge provides only an upper bound: three distinct positive horizons can additionally require a positive lower charge bound associated with the event-cosmological merger. For a test particle released from rest relative to a static observer, we derive the local charge-to-mass threshold for outward initial acceleration. Writing $\lambda=q/\mu$ and $z=Q/Q_{\rm ext}$, where $Q_{\rm ext}$ is the inner-event merger charge, the subextremal string-cloud threshold has infimum $1/z$, approached only at infinity. For the subextremal logarithmic profile, the threshold exceeds unity throughout the finite exterior and attains a finite global minimum. For $\alpha/M=0.4$ and $Q/M=1.2$, this minimum is $\lambda_*\simeq1.251125$. In a nondegenerate Kiselev static patch, the neutral balance radius is unique and radially unstable. Density matching supplies conditional profile normalizations, not observational fits. For the logarithmic profile, the integration parameter is distinguished from the mass combination inferred from circular motion. These results characterize geometric horizon bounds and test-particle dynamics; they do not establish a two-black-hole force law or a mechanism for cosmic acceleration, nor do they constitute a test of the Weak Gravity Conjecture.

</details>

Abstract: Below the gap scale of the AdS$_2$ throat, quantum fluctuations make the shear viscosity of near-extremal black branes grow as $(CT)^{-1/2}$ in the disk approximation of JT gravity, and the one-loop $\eta/s$ diverges. At finite extremal entropy $S_0$, level repulsion makes the DC viscosity vanish. A finite, universal viscosity survives in a double-scaling limit in which the probe frequency $\omega$ is held in a fixed ratio to the level-spacing scale $\omega_{\rm sp}\equiv e^{-S_0}/C$. At fixed $\omega$, with $\omega_{\rm sp}\ll\omega\ll e^{S_0/3}\omega_{\rm sp}$ the Schwarzian growth then saturates at $\eta_{\max}\simeq0.174\,(\omega/\omega_{\rm sp})\,\eta_{sc}$, where $\eta_{sc}$ is the semiclassical viscosity, and the viscosity decreases at lower temperatures. At the extremely low temperature scale $CT\sim e^{-2S_0/3}$ the genus expansion and the one-loop entropy computations completely break down. There we start probing the edge of the spectrum and the problem reduces exactly to the Airy kernel. We find that the ratio of the quenched averages of $\eta$ and $s$ is finite and positive at every non-zero temperature. We compare our results with explicit completions defined by the string equation, in which the spectrum is bounded from below. Abstract: We extend the algebro-geometric construction of filtered graded rings that encode both the Higgs branch and the Macdonald index of 4d $\mathcal{N}=2$ superconformal field theories to theories whose Schur sector contains fermionic generators. The rings are then $\mathbb{Z}_2$-graded-commutative, and the index is reproduced by the Hilbert series of a generalized arc space, whose defining ideal may contain relations among the generators and their derivatives. We study the free $\mathcal{N}=2$ vector multiplet, $\mathcal{N}=4$ super Yang--Mills with gauge algebras $\mathfrak{su}(2)$ and $\mathfrak{su}(3)$, the rank-one $\mathcal{N}=3$ SCFTs, and the $\widehat{E}_6(SU(2))$ SCFT. For the rank-one $\mathcal{N}=3$ SCFTs, the ideal is the sum of the Jacobian ideals of a single superpotential and of its two supersymmetry variations. For $\mathcal{N}=4$ super Yang--Mills, the ideal is similarly the sum of the Jacobian ideals of the components of a single $\mathcal{N}=4$ superfield, which is a singlet of the $\mathfrak{su}(2)$ flavor symmetry for gauge algebra $\mathfrak{su}(2)$, and a triplet for $\mathfrak{su}(3)$. The associated VOA for the $\widehat{E}_6(SU(2))$ theory is given by the Feigin--Feigin--Tipunin $\mathcal{A}(6)$ algebra. The arc space of Zhu's $C_2$-algebra of $\mathcal{A}(6)$ does not reproduce the vacuum character, and therefore $\mathcal{A}(6)$ is not classically free.   <summary>Details</summary>

Conclusion: Weyl一致性条件所允许的RG流本质上是梯度流，其障碍源于方案依赖性而非物理不可规避性；球面Ward恒等式提供了自然的物理方案以实现该梯度表示，统一了偶维d=2,4,6的A-定理与奇维F-定理框架。

Abstract: Weyl consistency conditions show that perturbative renormalisation group flows in two, four and six dimensions are almost gradient flows. The obstruction is an antisymmetric contribution to the metric on the space of couplings, which has resisted removal since the work of Jack and Osborn more than 35 years ago. We show that a conformal Ward identity on the sphere allows it to be removed by a choice of scheme. The beta function is then the gradient of a potential $A$ with respect to a symmetric metric, to all orders in perturbation theory. The Ward identity gives this result in any dimension $d$, in theories with and without a Weyl anomaly. At leading order the metric is fixed by the two-point functions of the perturbing operators, so unitarity makes it positive definite at weak coupling. At fixed points $A$ equals $\widetilde{F}=\sin\tfrac{\pi d}{2}\,\ln Z_{S^{d}}$, so the $\widetilde{F}$-theorem follows for perturbative flows to all loop orders. We show that sources of relevant operators, which on the sphere mix with the curvature, do not affect these results. In unitary theories the only exception is the source of a free scalar field near $d=6,10,\ldots\,$, so that for $\phi^3$ theory near six dimensions the gradient property remains open.

</details>

Motivation: 理解QCD在有限密度下的相结构，特别是禁闭-去禁闭相变机制；将AdS/QCD模型推广至含高斯-邦内特曲率修正的引力背景，以探索高阶引力效应对相变温度、相共存及强子谱的影响。

Method: 采用五维Einstein-Maxwell-Gauss-Bonnet引力理论建模，引入夸克物质的背反应；将去禁闭相设为带电GB黑洞，禁闭相设为红外截断的热力学带电AdS时空；通过红外边界条件确定规范场值；利用热力学稳定性判据（自由能比较）确定相变线；对禁闭相中ρ介子谱采用WKB近似分析其密度依赖性。

Result: 获得固定夸克化学势和固定夸克数密度下的相变线解析解；发现负GB耦合提升相变温度并扩展禁闭相至更高密度；零密度下满足因果律约束的GB参数可使相变温度接近格点QCD结果；在致密禁闭相中，ρ介子及其径向激发态质量随密度单调增加，核密度下质量上升约1%；WKB分析表明该效应源于有效禁闭区域尺寸缩短。

Conclusion: 高斯-邦内特引力修正可显著调控AdS/QCD模型的相结构与强子谱，尤其在有限密度下提供与格点数据更兼容的相变温度，并揭示禁闭尺度随密度演化的物理图像；该框架为研究致密QCD物质（如中子星内部）提供了可计算的全息模型。

Abstract: We study the confinement/deconfinement transition of the hard wall model of AdS/QCD at finite quark density in five-dimensional Einstein--Maxwell--Gauss--Bonnet gravity, with the back-reaction of the quark matter on the geometry included. The deconfined phase is a charged Gauss--Bonnet black hole, and the confined phase is thermally charged AdS cut off in the infrared. The infrared value of the gauge field, which Lee, Park and Sin fixed by requiring a consistent quark number, becomes a function of the Gauss--Bonnet coupling. We obtain the transition line in closed form at fixed quark chemical potential and at fixed quark number density. At fixed density the transition passes through a region where the two phases coexist. A negative Gauss--Bonnet coupling raises the transition temperature and extends the confined phase to higher density. A value inside the causality bound gives a transition temperature at zero density close to the lattice value. In the dense confined phase we compute the masses and decay constants of the $\rho$ meson and its radial excitations. All masses increase with density. A WKB analysis relates the increase to a shorter effective size of the confining region, and at nuclear density the $\rho$ mass rises by about one percent.

</details>


### [103] [Finite-Temperature Stabilization of a KKLT Modulus in Three-Dimensional Lattice Simulations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29000)
*Saya Mahmoudi,Furkan Utku Biber,Hyukjung Kim,Heeseung Zoe*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文通过三维Langevin格点模拟研究KKLT模的宇宙学稳定化，发现即使空间平均模被俘获在势垒内，长波涨落仍可通过快子放大导致局域区域越过势垒，表明平均俘获不保证全域稳定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解KKLT机制中模场在早期宇宙热环境下的动力学稳定性，特别是空间非均匀性与随机涨落对真空捕获可靠性的影响。

Method: 三维Langevin格点模拟，包含动态热浴、耗散项、空间梯度及随机噪声；辅以线性Langevin理论解析和离散化收敛性检验。

Result: 识别出临界俘获附近的狭窄随机层，在此区域内越过势垒的概率与离散化无关；长波模式发生tachyonic放大，导致局域穿越而整体平均仍回滚。

Conclusion: 空间平均模的俘获并非全域稳定的充分条件；必须考虑空间涨落与非平衡随机动力学对KKLT真空选择的真实影响。

Abstract: We investigate the cosmological stabilization of a KKLT modulus through three-dimensional Langevin lattice simulations incorporating an evolving thermal bath, dissipation, spatial gradients, and stochastic fluctuations. While the spatially averaged modulus closely follows homogeneous evolution away from the capture threshold, near-critical trajectories linger near the unstable potential barrier for approximately one e-fold, allowing long-wavelength fluctuations to undergo tachyonic amplification and drive localized regions across the barrier even as the mean field turns back toward the stabilized vacuum. Linear Langevin theory reproduces the fluctuation growth, while convergence tests identify a narrow stochastic layer in which the barrier-crossing fraction shows no systematic dependence on the discretization at a fixed effective distance from the threshold. These results demonstrate that capture of the spatially averaged modulus does not guarantee that every spatial region remains within the stabilizing barrier.

</details>


### [104] [Exact stress-tensor fluctuations from conformal symmetry and black hole radiation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29060)
*José Navarro-Salas,Roberto Balbinot,Alessandro Fabbri*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究二维共形引力中应力张量分量 $T_{\pm\pm}$ 的局域涨落，利用共形对称性与算符乘积展开（OPE）定义 $T_{\pm\pm}^2$，通过共形映射至局域惯性坐标系得到其方差闭式解；涨落相对大小为 $\mathcal{O}(1/c)$，其中 $c$ 为中心荷；在Schwarzschild黑洞不同位置（无穷远、视界）给出具体数值结果，并指出 Möbius-协变点分裂（$\alpha = -3/10$）使内外流涨落一致，从而支持大 $c$ 下半经典近似的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 验证二维半经典引力的适用性——其成立要求应力张量涨落相对于期望值足够小；而共形对称性在二维s波霍金辐射中精确给出均值，但涨落行为尚需系统刻画。

Method: 利用二维共形场论中应力张量的OPE定义 $T_{\pm\pm}^2$（含点分裂参数 $\alpha$），结合共形映射到局域惯性坐标系下的变换律，导出 $\langle (\delta T_{\pm\pm})^2 \rangle$ 的解析表达式；特别处理视界处的坐标奇异性与不同 $\alpha$ 对应的正规化方案。

Result: 相对涨落 $\sigma_{T_{\pm\pm}} / \langle T_{\pm\pm} \rangle \sim \mathcal{O}(1/c)$；无穷远处霍金通量涨落为 $22/(5c)$；视界处出射通量涨落为 $-44\alpha/(3c)$，入射通量涨落为 $(238+720\alpha)/(5c)$；当 $\alpha = -3/10$（Möbius协变正规化）时，三者统一为 $22/(5c)$。

Conclusion: 在大中心荷极限下，应力张量涨落被压低至 $1/c$ 量级，证实二维共形半经典引力在霍金辐射背景下自洽；Möbius协变点分裂是物理上最自然的正规化选择，确保视界与无穷远涨落一致性。

Abstract: Semiclassical gravity is sourced by the expectation value of the stress tensor, and it is reliable only if the fluctuations around this value are small. In two dimensions, conformal symmetry captures the essential physics of Hawking radiation in its s-wave sector and determines the mean value exactly, for any background and any quantum state. We show that it also determines the local fluctuations of the null components $T_{\pm\pm}$. The square of the stress tensor is defined by the operator product expansion, up to a multiple $\alpha$ of $\partial_\pm^2T_{\pm\pm}$ that labels point-splitting prescriptions. Its transformation law under the conformal map to locally inertial coordinates gives the variance in closed form. The relative fluctuation scales as $1/c$, with $c$ the central charge. For the Schwarzschild black hole it is $22/5c$ for the Hawking flux at infinity, that of flat-space thermal radiation. At the horizon it is $-44\alpha/3c$ for the outgoing flux and $(238+720\alpha)/5c$ for the ingoing flux that drives the backreaction. Both reduce to $22/5c$ for $\alpha=-3/10$, which corresponds to the Möbius-covariant prescription. For large $c$ these results support the semiclassical approximation.

</details>


### [105] [The Generalized Second Law from Fluctuation Theorems in a Quantum Reference Frame](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29415)
*Feng-Li Lin,Sayid Mondal*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文通过引入引力荷作为量子钟，构建了模交叉积，并利用量子信道涨落定理（FT）在固定半有限代数上证明了黑洞外部的广义第二定律（GSL），给出了三种互补的动力学表述：分离式幺正驱动与弛豫、时间局域描述、以及两时刻意外性涨落定理；特别指出幺正性并非GSL成立的必要条件，而仅是该FT证明的充分条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 突破Wall早期基于半经典几何与逐次视界切片代数的GSL证明框架，将状态演化置于固定的‘ dressed exterior algebra’上，以实现更普适、更符合量子引力协变性的熵增原理表述。

Method: 采用模理论（modular theory）与状态–算符对偶性，构造以引力荷为量子钟的模交叉积（modular crossed product），定义含时密度算符；结合量子信道涨落定理（quantum channel fluctuation theorem）、相对熵收缩性、物理过程第一定律（horizon absorption ↔ area gain）及数据处理不等式，建立三类动力学框架下的GSL证明。

Result: 严格证明了在固定半有限代数下、经幺正驱动与弛豫的黑洞外部满足GSL；导出有限时钟宽度修正项；确立幺正演化（unital dressed evolution）下GSL的显式熵界；揭示幺正性是该FT路径下GSL可证的充分但非必要条件。

Conclusion: GSL是开放黑洞外部量子动力学的普适热力学约束，其成立不依赖于特定半经典近似或代数嵌套结构，而根植于模理论、量子信息流与引力荷时序结构的深层统一；有限时钟效应可系统量化，且非幺正弛豫亦可满足GSL。

Abstract: We prove the generalized second law (GSL) for black-hole exteriors undergoing unitary driving and relaxation on a fixed semifinite algebra. Wall's influential proof assumes semiclassical geometry and restricts fixed parent states to algebras of successive horizon cuts. We instead evolve the state on a fixed dressed exterior algebra. With complete characteristic data, the same semiclassical exterior supports both descriptions, analogous to the Heisenberg and Schrödinger pictures through state--operator duality. Using a gravitational charge as a quantum clock, we construct a modular crossed product with density operators whose trace entropy reproduces generalized entropy up to clock contributions. Our approach parallels thermodynamics in regulated field theory and formulates the GSL through a quantum channel fluctuation theorem (FT) for the open exterior. We develop three complementary formulations under specified dynamical assumptions. Separated unitary driving and relaxation combine modular work, the physical-process first law relating horizon absorption to area gain, and relative-entropy contraction through data processing. A time-local description accommodates simultaneous unitary driving and dissipation. A two-time surprisal FT treats the complete channel, identifies the nonunital correction, and yields a GSL bound for unital dressed evolution without resolving intermediate dynamics. Relaxation that does not mix distinct dressed-charge sectors and preserves the full dressed reference state is unital. Unitality is sufficient for this FT proof but is not necessary for the GSL along a particular evolution. The entropy bounds establish the GSL in a controlled slow-clock limit and quantify finite-width clock corrections.

</details>


### [106] [Symmetry resolved spread complexity for BMN matrix model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29425)
*Dibakar Roychowdhury*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究BMN矩阵模型在SO(6)标量扇区U(1)全局对称性下的对称性分解spread复杂度，对比低温禁闭相与高温去禁闭相中cTFD态的返回振幅、谱矩、Lanczos系数及早期spread复杂度行为，并建立其与全息对偶几何（LM droplet vs. 带电D0-膜黑洞）的对应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解量子混沌、热化与对称性分解复杂度在AdS/CFT框架下的微观实现，特别是通过BMN模型这一可控可解的近BPS极限探查对称性如何影响算符增长与信息扩散。

Method: 将时间演化的带电热场双重态(cTFD)按U(1)荷q正交分解；计算各荷扇区的返回振幅、谱矩、三对角Lanczos系数{a_n^{(q)}, b_n^{(q)}}；提取早期spread复杂度C^{(q)}(t)，并结合有效哈密顿量与谱密度建模（低温位移谐振子 vs. 高温热高斯谱）。

Result: 低温相：Lanczos系数呈线性对角a_n^{(q)}与√n型非对角b_n^{(q)}增长，复杂度增长受热抑制且不满足等分；存在早时正值性界ΔC(t)≥0；高温相：a_n^{(q)}恒定、b_n^{(q)}普适√n标度，C^{(q)}(t)以σ_E²(q,T)t²启动；两相分别全息对应LM droplet几何中的相干弦涨落与带电D0-膜黑洞视界附近的耗散型量子混沌。

Conclusion: 对称性分解spread复杂度能清晰区分BMN模型中由能隙与热激发主导的不同动力学机制，并为全息复杂度-几何关系提供精细的荷分辨检验工具。

Abstract: We investigate the symmetry-resolved spread complexity of the Berenstein-Maldacena-Nastase (BMN) matrix model under a global $U(1)$ symmetry acting within the transverse $SO(6)$ scalar sector. Decomposing the time-evolved charged thermofield double (cTFD) state into orthogonal fixed-charge sectors, we examine the return amplitude, spectral moments, tridiagonal Lanczos coefficients, and early-time spread complexity across the low-temperature confined ($\beta\mu \gg 1$) and finite-temperature deconfined ($T \gg \mu$) phases. In the low-temperature gapped regime, effective sector dynamics map onto decoupled, displaced harmonic oscillators with a Casimir-like zero-point offset, yielding factorized moments, linear drift in diagonal Lanczos coefficients $a_n^{(q)}$, and sub-linear $\sqrt{n}$ off-diagonal growth with thermally suppressed initial hopping. Charge-dependent Boltzmann weights, vacuum shifts, and degeneracies break complexity equipartition, while inter-sector coherence and charge dispersion enforce an early-time positivity bound $\Delta C(t) = C_{\text{total}}(t) - \bar{C}(t) \ge 0$. In the \emph{finite}-temperature continuum phase, modeling the planar large-$N$ spectral density as a thermal Gaussian gives constant diagonal coefficients $a_n^{(q)} = \bar{E}_q$, universal square-root scaling $b_n^{(q)} = \sigma_E \sqrt{n}$, and a quadratic spread launch $\mathcal{C}^{(q)}(t) = \sigma_E^2(q,T)\,t^2 + \mathcal{O}(t^4)$ governed by the backreacted sector energy variance. Holographically, these regimes distinguish coherent string fluctuations in horizonless Lin-Maldacena (LM) droplet geometries from dissipative scrambling near charged ten-dimensional black D0-brane horizons.

</details>


### [107] [Scattering amplitudes in AdS$_5\times$ S$^5$ from the pure spinor superstring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29622)
*Luis F. Alday,Thiago Fleury,Tobias Hansen,Lucas N. S. Martins,Dennis Zavaleta*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文提出了纯自旋超弦在AdS₅×S⁵背景中零模积分的BRST不变方案，解决了该背景下振幅计算的核心障碍，并首次直接通过世界面计算了半BPS超引力态的二点与三点振幅，其归一化三点振幅精确复现了平面N=4超杨-米尔斯理论结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 克服纯自旋器形式下AdS₅×S⁵背景中零模积分缺乏BRST不变定义这一长期障碍，以支持高精度超弦振幅计算并检验AdS/CFT对应。

Method: 构造BRST闭合的零模测度，引入非平直空间特有贡献；采用picture-changing算子或非极小正则化器实现规范不变性；适用于任意外线态与重数的亏格零振幅。

Result: 首次完成AdS₅×S₅中半BPS超引力态的世界面二点与三点振幅直接计算；归一化三点函数严格匹配N=4 SYM平面极限下的强耦合预言。

Conclusion: 该BRST不变零模处理方案为纯自旋器形式下AdS/CFT高阶检验（如四点函数、量子修正）建立了可靠的世界面框架。

Abstract: We formulate a BRST-invariant prescription for the integration of zero modes in the pure spinor superstring on $AdS_5\times S^5$. The measure includes contributions absent in flat space and admits a description in terms of picture changing operators or a non-minimal regulator. The prescription is valid for genus-zero amplitudes with arbitrary multiplicity and external states and removes a central obstacle to amplitude computations in this background. We use it to perform the first direct worldsheet computation of two and three-point amplitudes of half-BPS supergravity states. The properly normalised three-point amplitudes exactly reproduce the results of planar ${\mathcal N}=4$ super Yang-Mills theory.

</details>

Method: 基于变分原理构造边界抵消项，要求离壳涨落满足由渐近运动方程导出的衰减条件；通过离壳对偶化RR二形式势，实现固定D1-膜电荷的系综约束。

Conclusion: 该边界抵消项确保了超引力描述与IKKT矩阵模型在真空能量层面的定量一致性，验证了该全息对偶在非微扰、极化背景下的自洽性，并为研究D-brane量子化与全息重整化提供了新工具。

Abstract: We construct a boundary counterterm for the on-shell action of type IIB supergravity solutions dual to vacua of the polarized IKKT matrix model. The renormalized on-shell action of the supersymmetric saddles reproduces the matrix- model result once we pass to the ensemble of fixed D1-brane charges, which we implement off-shell by dualizing the RR two-form potential.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究在固定冯·诺依曼熵条件下，纠缠涨落（由容量表征）所能达到的最小值，即容量与熵之间的基本约束关系。

Method: 基于具标量势的全息共形界面模型，利用背景Einstein方程分析耦合引力响应的组成（输运项减去正定标量贡献），并结合长Janus喉部渐近几何进行分析。

Result: 证明了容量c_cap满足c_eff ≤ c_cap ≤ c_eff²/c_LR，其中c_eff为有效中心荷，c_LR为左-右中心荷差；发现长Janus喉可使c_cap在有限c_eff下无界增长，而标量响应仍保持能隙。

Abstract: How small can entanglement fluctuations be at fixed entropy? For a class of holographic conformal interfaces supported by a scalar with a potential, we prove $c_\text{eff}\leq c_\text{cap}\leq c_\text{eff}^2/c_{LR}$, relating entropy, capacity, and energy transmission. The coupled gravitational response is a transport term minus a positive scalar contribution; the background Einstein equation bounds that subtraction. A long Janus throat allows unbounded capacity at a finite entropy coefficient while the scalar response remains gapped.

</details>


### [110] [Lie Algebra Cohomology and Seiberg-Witten Theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29713)
*Ahsan Z. Khan*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文计算了纯N=2超对称SU(2)规范理论中微扰全纯-拓扑局域算符代数，证明其为由中心荷为零的Virasoro元X与权为3的奇初等元Y生成的泊松顶点代数，且满足X²=0；等价地，计算了相对李代数上同调H*(sl₂[z,w]/(w²), sl₂)及SL₂/PGL₂相容Satake范畴中单位对象的自Ext群。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 验证作者先前关于N=2纯规范理论中全纯-拓扑局域算符代数结构的猜想，并建立其与相对李代数上同调及相干Satake范畴中Ext群之间的精确对应。

Method: 利用仿射直线上的构型空间上的因子化结构、深度论证（depth argument），以及系数代数C[z,w]/(w²)的两类形变。

Result: 确认该算符代数为Poisson vertex algebra，由Virasoro元X（c=0）和奇初等元Y（Δ=3）生成，满足单一关系X²=0；同时给出H*(sl₂[z,w]/(w²), sl₂)的具体结构及Satake范畴中单位对象的Ext代数。

Conclusion: 纯N=2 SU(2)规范理论的微扰全纯-拓扑局域算符代数具有明确的Poisson vertex algebra结构，该结果统一了量子场论、表示论与几何朗兰兹纲领中的多个代数结构。

Abstract: We compute the algebra of perturbative holomorphic-topological local operators of pure $\mathcal N=2$ gauge theory with gauge group $SU(2)$, proving a conjecture of the author: it is the Poisson vertex algebra generated by a Virasoro element $X$ of central charge zero and an odd primary $Y$ of weight three, subject only to $X^2=0$. Equivalently, we compute the relative Lie algebra cohomology $H^*(\mathfrak{sl}_2[z,w]/(w^2),\mathfrak{sl}_2)$ and the self-Ext groups of the unit in the coherent Satake category for $SL_2$ and $PGL_2$. The proof uses factorization on configuration spaces of points on the affine line, a depth argument, and two deformations of the coefficient algebra $\mathbb C[z,w]/(w^2)$.

</details>


### [111] [Boundary spectroscopy of hidden near-horizon reflection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30092)
*Yiling Chen,Jiayue Yang,Zhen Zhong,Robert B. Mann,Jie Jiang,Ming Zhang*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了旋转BTZ黑洞外部的标量场在近视界处共转被动壁的边界响应，发现其频谱由Airy滤波器给出，且正能量条件排除了增长模；边界观测显示该壁具有恒定阻抗并可确定其位置，表明其表现为反射壁而非完美吸收体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典黑洞视界完全吸收所有入射物质，任何偏离完全吸收的行为理论上应被体空间（bulk）所隐藏；本文旨在探究在AdS/CFT框架下，近视界边界上能否通过响应函数探测到这种'非完全吸收'的物理效应。

Method: 基于旋转Bañados-Teitelboim-Zanelli（BTZ）黑洞背景，求解标量场的精确边界响应函数；引入共转被动壁模型，利用Airy函数分析其频谱特性，并结合正能量条件约束模稳定性；通过谱函数正交分解提取壁的阻抗与位置参数。

Result: 得到精确的边界响应解析表达式，其频谱由Airy滤波器刻画；证明在任意频率带内，响应唯一确定壁的恒定阻抗与径向位置，二者以谱函数加权正交方式出现；正能量条件确保无不稳定（增长）模式；边界观测等效于一个反射壁，而理想吸收行为呈指数级隐藏。

Conclusion: 即使在经典黑洞视界附近，边界观测仍可分辨非完全吸收结构（如共转被动壁），其物理参数（阻抗、位置）可由响应谱唯一重构；这挑战了‘吸收偏差必被体隐藏’的直观图像，为AdS/CFT中视界微观结构的边界探针提供了新范式。

Abstract: A classical black hole horizon absorbs completely, and any deviation would be hidden in the bulk. For a scalar field in a rotating Bañados-Teitelboim-Zanelli exterior, we derive an exact boundary response of a corotating passive wall near the horizon. An Airy filter gives its spectrum, and positive energy excludes growing modes. On any frequency band the response identifies a constant wall impedance and the wall position, which enter orthogonally with weights determined by the spectral function. The boundary thus sees a reflecting wall, whereas a perfect absorber is exponentially hidden.

</details>


### [112] [Charged spin-$\frac32$ in a chiral Feshbach--Villars model: hyperbolic propagation, Landau-level instability, and exceptional points](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30225)
*Haryanto M. Siahaan*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了在(3/2,0)⊕(0,3/2)表示下、含自由g因子的Pauli耦合二阶波动方程，其Feshbach–Villars形式为16分量伪厄米哈密顿系统；发现自旋-j Landau谱保持实数的充要条件是|g|j ≤ 1；在g=2、j=3/2时，磁场临界值|qB|=m²/2处出现二阶例外点并丧失正定守恒二次型；引入电势扰动后，在特定磁场区间内出现非实本征值分支，表明谱不稳定性，但未确立费米子量子化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究高自旋（特别是j=3/2）相对论性波动方程在电磁场中的谱性质与物理可接受性，尤其是伪厄米性、实谱条件、例外点结构及扰动稳定性，以厘清高自旋场论中谱不稳定性与双曲性破缺的本质区别。

Method: 采用Feshbach–Villars分解将二阶Pauli耦合方程转化为十六分量伪厄米哈密顿系统；利用协变Landau规范求解均匀磁场下的精确谱；分析例外点（EP2）的出现条件与守恒二次型的正定性丧失；对附加高斯阻尼静电势作一阶微扰分析，投影至最低两态自旋流形并解析V±iC结构。

Result: （1）自旋-j Landau谱实性的充要条件为|g|j ≤ 1；（2）g=2、j=3/2时，|qB|=m²/2为二阶例外点阈值，且此时正定守恒二次型消失；（3）加入电势扰动后，在m²/6 < |qB| < m²/2区间内，存在非实本征值分支（除特殊势阱宽度外）；（4）j=1/2、g=2满足Dirac实谱判据，而g=1/j时该扰动下无一阶虚部修正。

Conclusion: 谱不稳定性（非实本征值）可独立于双曲性破缺发生；当前模型虽具伪厄米结构和部分实谱区域，但缺乏协变单粒子物理解释与费米子量子化方案；结果强调g因子与自旋匹配对高自旋场论物理自洽性的决定性作用。

Abstract: We study a Pauli-coupled second-order wave equation on $(\frac32,0)\oplus(0,\frac32)$ with a free gyromagnetic ratio $g$. Its Feshbach--Villars form is a sixteen-component pseudo-Hermitian Hamiltonian system with lightlike covariant characteristics in smooth electromagnetic backgrounds. The solution space is chirality doubled; a covariant physical single-species reduction is not assumed. The exact spin-$j$ Landau spectrum remains real at every field strength precisely when $|g|j\le1$. At $g=2$, the critical spin-$\frac32$ channel reaches zero at $|qB|=m^2/2$, where each chiral block develops a second-order exceptional point. This threshold also marks the loss of a time-independent positive-definite conserved quadratic form for the channel. We also include a bounded, Gaussian-damped harmonic electrostatic potential and its associated electric field. The complete first-order problem on a degenerate manifold reduces to $V\pm iC$, with $V$ and $C$ the projected potential and spin--electric coupling. On the lowest two-state spin-$\frac32$ manifold, nonreal eigenvalue branches arise for $m^2/6<|qB|<m^2/2$ at sufficiently small nonzero perturbation amplitude, except at a special well range where the first-order splitting vanishes. The spin-$\frac12$, $g=2$ limit satisfies the Dirac spectral-reality check, whereas $g=1/j$ has no imaginary first-order correction for this perturbation. These results separate spectral instability from loss of hyperbolicity; they do not establish a physical fermionic quantization.

</details>


### [113] [Matter-decorated trumpets in double-scaled SYK](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30296)
*Matteo Beccaria*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文在sine-dilaton引力及其边界对偶DSSYK模型中，构造了携带标量物质场（维度Δ）的双喇叭（double trumpet）虫洞振幅，超越了Jackiw–Teitelboim极限；通过ETH双矩阵模型定义物质修饰虫洞，从带物质插入的端界膜（EOW brane）迹出发构建物质修饰喇叭，并严格证明其按弦数（chords）分扇区精确拼接为虫洞，权重为正，包含弦的扭结、缠绕及绕颈闭圈贡献；喇叭解以Rogers多项式的积分变换给出，权重亦得闭式解；在JT极限下各自退化为已知结果，在经典极限下喇叭呈现纯引力sine-dilaton喇叭乘以物质测地线边界贡献的预期形式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解非JT型稀疏稀释引力理论（如sine-dilaton gravity）中物质场如何影响拓扑虫洞振幅的构造，特别是突破JT极限下物质修饰双喇叭无法直接拼接的障碍，并验证DSSYK作为其全息对偶的完备性。

Method: 基于DSSYK模型的ETH双矩阵框架，将物质场建模为高斯随机矩阵；利用端界膜（EOW brane）轨迹算符插入物质算符构造物质修饰喇叭；通过弦图（chord diagram）技术按穿过虫洞颈的弦数分扇区展开，结合组合权重（含Brauer代数结构描述的扭结与缠绕）实现精确拼接；解析求解喇叭振幅为Rogers多项式的Mellin-Barnes型积分变换。

Result: 首次在sine-dilaton引力/DSSYK框架中给出非JT极限下物质修饰双喇叭的严格构造：（1）物质修饰喇叭振幅以Rogers多项式变换闭式表达；（2）拼接权重为正定、可显式计算，编码弦的拓扑缠绕与闭圈动力学；（3）JT极限下喇叭与权重各自独立退化至已知JT结果；（4）经典极限下喇叭渐近行为匹配半经典bulk几何预言——即纯引力喇叭乘以物质测地线边界因子。

Conclusion: 物质修饰虫洞的拼接结构在sine-dilaton引力中具有普适组合本质，由DSSYK的弦图动力学完全控制；该构造证实DSSYK不仅是JT的推广，更是sine-dilaton全息的自洽实现；Rogers多项式作为新正交基揭示了稀疏稀释引力中更深层的q-变形对称性。

Abstract: In sine-dilaton gravity, the double trumpet, the wormhole amplitude without matter, is obtained by gluing two trumpets along a closed geodesic of discrete length and summing over this length. When the wormhole carries the propagator of a matter field of dimension $\Delta$, joining the two asymptotic boundaries and crossing the neck, such a gluing is known only in the Jackiw--Teitelboim (JT) limit. We construct the gluing beyond JT in double-scaled SYK (DSSYK), the proposed boundary dual of sine-dilaton gravity, whose disk amplitudes it reproduces. In this context, we adopt the definition of the matter-decorated wormhole in terms of the ETH two-matrix model with Gaussian matter. We build matter-decorated trumpets from an end-of-the-world brane trace with a matter insertion, and prove that the wormhole is glued from them exactly, sector by sector in the number of chords through the neck, with a positive weight that accounts for the twists and windings of these chords and for the closed loops around the neck. Trumpets and weight are obtained in closed form, the former as transforms of Rogers polynomials, and we discuss the uniqueness of the construction. In the JT limit, both reproduce the known gluing separately, and not only in combination. In the classical limit, each trumpet takes the form expected in the bulk: a pure-gravity sine-dilaton trumpet dressed by the factor that a probe matter geodesic contributes at its boundary.

</details>


### [114] [Rotating Black Holes from Scaling Transformations in Horava Gravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30337)
*Deniz O. Devecioglu,Mu-In Park*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了低能Hořava引力中改变解物理参数的尺度变换，提出了一种简单尺度变换，可从已知相对论解生成旋转黑洞解，并给出了Doran型和Kerr-Schild型两类具体解；二者均为穿视界解，但其零视界非Killing视界；奇点结构不同，但均保护因果性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 构建低能Hořava引力中具有物理意义的旋转黑洞解，以检验该理论在强场、动力学背景下的可行性与一致性，并与广义相对论预言进行对比。

Method: 利用尺度对称性（scaling transformation）作用于广义相对论中的已知轴对称真空解（如Kerr解），导出满足低能Hořava引力场方程的对应解；具体构造Doran坐标系与Kerr-Schild形式的度规，并分析其几何与因果结构。

Result: 得到了两类新的旋转黑洞解：'Doran-like'与'Kerr-Schild-like'解；二者均具穿视界性质，零视界非Killing视界；奇点结构不同（如曲率发散行为或坐标覆盖范围差异），但均无闭合类时曲线（CTC），即因果性得以保持。

Conclusion: 低能Hořava引力允许存在物理上合理的旋转黑洞解，其因果结构稳健，支持该理论作为洛伦兹破缺紫外完备候选者的自洽性；尺度变换是构造非相对论性引力理论精确解的有效工具。

Abstract: We study the scaling transformations that change the physical parameters of solutions in low-energy Horava gravity. In particular, we present a simple scaling transformation that generates rotating black-hole solutions in low-energy Horava gravity from known relativistic solutions. As explicit examples, we obtain "Doran-like" and "Kerr-Schild-like" rotating solutions in Horava gravity. We find that both solutions are horizon-penetrating, but their null horizons are not Killing horizons. We also find that, although their singularity structures differ from each other, chronology is protected in both solutions, as in the previously obtained Kerr-like solutions in Boyer-Lindquist coordinates.

</details>


### [115] [Non-invertible Duality Defects in Four-Dimensional $\mathbb{Z}_N$ Lattice Gauge Theories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30489)
*Satoshi Yamaguchi*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究四维Z_N格点规范理论中的对偶性，构造了实现这些对偶性的拓扑界面，并在自对偶耦合处将其识别为非可逆对称性的对偶缺陷；同时分析了中心对称缺陷及其与对偶界面的交点，导出了若干交叉关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解高维格点规范理论中对偶性与拓扑界面之间的联系，特别是非可逆对称性在量子场论中的实现机制。

Method: 基于广义plaquette作用量构造Z_N格点规范理论，通过赋予每个基本构型权重并验证其拓扑不变性，定义对偶性实现的拓扑界面；结合中心对称缺陷与界面交点分析，推导交叉关系。

Result: 成功构造了实现Z_N理论对偶性的拓扑界面；证明其在自对偶点成为非可逆对偶缺陷；系统刻画了中心对称缺陷与对偶界面的junction结构及相应F-符号和交叉方程。

Conclusion: 四维Z_N格点规范理论中的对偶性可由拓扑界面精确实现，且在自对偶点提升为非可逆对称性；中心对称与对偶性之间存在受拓扑约束的非平凡耦合结构。

Abstract: We consider four-dimensional $\mathbb{Z}_N$ lattice gauge theories with generalized plaquette actions. These theories are known to exhibit dualities. We construct topological interfaces implementing these dualities by assigning a weight to each building block and proving topological invariance. At self-dual couplings, these interfaces become duality defects representing non-invertible symmetries. We also study center symmetry defects and their junctions with the duality interfaces and derive several crossing relations.

</details>


### [116] [Dirichlet walls beyond JT gravity and their quantum group interpretation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30567)
*Luca Griguolo,Jacopo Papalini,Lorenzo Russo,Domenico Seminara,Alex Tarana*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文对sinh稀释子引力进行正则量子化，得到基于q-超几何函数的宇宙波函数，并揭示其与量子群U_q(sl(2,R))约化Casimir及模双代数散射解的深刻联系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在AdS₂/CFT₁背景下，拓展Jackiw–Teitelboim（JT）引力的量子结构，探索有限截断下引力理论的可重整化量子实现及其代数结构。

Method: 采用正则量子化方法，在有限Dirichlet边界条件下构造sinh稀释子引力的约化相空间；引入两维类空测地线长度及其共轭动量作为基本变量；导出有限差分薛定谔方程；识别其解为q-超几何函数，并建立其与U_q(sl(2,R))约化Casimir及模双代数矩阵元的对应。

Result: 获得以q-超几何函数表征的宇宙波函数；证明有限截断哈密顿量等于U_q(sl(2,R))的约化Casimir；两个散射解被识别为模双代数U_q(sl(2,R))⊗U_{\tilde q}(sl(2,R))的双曲矩阵元；唯一在长度为零处正则的组合即为宇宙波函数。

Conclusion: sinh稀释子引力在有限截断下展现出精确可解的量子结构，其波函数构成JT引力超几何解的q-变形，且自然嵌入量子群表示论框架，为AdS₂量子引力提供了新的代数几何诠释。

Abstract: We canonically quantize sinh dilaton gravity with finite Dirichlet boundary conditions. The reduced phase space is formulated in terms of the length of a two-sided spacelike geodesic in an effective AdS$_2$ geometry and its conjugate momentum. Canonical quantization leads to a finite-difference Schrödinger equation whose wavefunctions of the universe are given by $q$-hypergeometric functions. These furnish a quantum deformation of the hypergeometric wavefunctions of JT gravity. We further show that the finite cutoff Hamiltonian derived from the bulk analysis admits a natural interpretation as the reduced Casimir of $U_q(\mathfrak{sl}(2,\mathbb{R}))$ acting on hyperbolic matrix elements. The two scattering wave solutions are hyperbolic matrix elements of the modular double $U_q(\mathfrak{sl}(2,\mathbb R))\otimes U_{\tilde q}(\mathfrak{sl}(2,\mathbb R))$, and the wavefunction of the universe is their unique combination regular at vanishing length.

</details>


### [117] [Near-boundary and near-horizon momentum--Krylov response in noncommutative-inspired BTZ spacetime](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31106)
*F. A. Brito,F. G. Costa,J. A. V. Campos*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了洛伦兹平滑、非对易启发的BTZ黑洞中下落试探粒子的径向动量，并将其作为Krylov复杂度的增长率；发现平滑参数改变早期增长系数与晚期指数（表面引力），二者依赖不同，导致响应不可简单缩放；临界平滑时视界半径与表面引力以1/2和3/2幂律消失；边界应力张量与BTZ一致，但Fefferman–Graham展开z³项及边界锚定测地线长度不同。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解非对易几何修正对黑洞内部动力学及量子复杂度演化的影响，特别是探究平滑（smeared）质量分布如何改变Krylov复杂度的时间演化特征及其与标准BTZ黑洞的偏离。

Method: 基于Lorentzian-smearing的非对易BTZ度规，计算大质量试探粒子的固有径向动量p_r(τ)；将其定义为动量-Krylov复杂度K(τ)的时间导数dK/dτ；解析推导早期（τ→0）与晚期（τ→∞）渐近行为，并数值验证；对比高斯质量分布与临界平滑极限下的几何量（如x₊, κ）标度律；分析AdS₃/CFT₂对应下的边界应力张量与Fefferman–Graham展开系数。

Result: 1) 平滑BTZ中Krylov复杂度初始增长率为F'_α(X)/4，晚期指数为表面引力κ = F'_α(x_+)/2，二者对平滑参数α依赖不同；2) 远距离释放时早期趋近BTZ，晚期仍显著偏离；3) 同Hawking温度的BTZ仅能复现响应至~10%精度，残差源于早/晚期不匹配；4) 临界平滑时x₊ ∝ α^{1/2}, κ ∝ α^{3/2}；5) 边界应力张量T_{ij}^{(CFT)}与BTZ相同，但z³项c₃及边界锚定测地线长度ΔL ≠ 0。

Conclusion: 非对易平滑不仅修改黑洞内部几何，更在复杂度动力学中引入不可缩放的新尺度：早期由探测点X决定，晚期由视界处表面引力决定，二者解耦；这表明Krylov复杂度对bulk短程结构（如质量分布）具有敏感性，超越传统热力学量（如T_H）所能刻画的范畴；结果支持复杂度-体积/动作对偶在非对易背景下的鲁棒性，同时揭示其几何起源的细微分层。

Abstract: We compute the proper radial momentum of a massive probe falling into a Lorentzian-smeared, noncommutative-inspired BTZ black hole at fixed asymptotic mass, and use it as the growth rate of momentum--Krylov complexity. In the BTZ limit the accumulated complexity is $\mathcal{K}_0 = X(\cosh\tau - 1)$. With smearing, the initial growth coefficient is $F'_\alpha(X)/4$, while the late-time exponent is the surface gravity $F'_\alpha(x_+)/2$. The two depend differently on the smearing parameter, so the deformed response is not a rescaled BTZ curve; we show this analytically and confirm it numerically. For a distant release point the early response approaches BTZ, whereas the late-time rate does not. A BTZ black hole with the same Hawking temperature reproduces the response to within about ten percent, and the remaining difference is set by the early/late mismatch. A Gaussian mass profile gives the same picture. The horizon radius and the surface gravity vanish at the critical smearing with exponents $1/2$ and $3/2$. At fixed mass the boundary stress tensor coincides with that of BTZ, but the $z^3$ Fefferman--Graham coefficient and the lengths of boundary-anchored geodesics do not.

</details>


### [118] [Nonabelianization map for the $W_{3,c=2}$ conformal blocks. Part I](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31232)
*Qianyu Hao*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个广义的非阿贝尔化映射，用于从三重分支覆盖曲面	ilde{C}上的Heisenberg块构造Riemann曲面C上c=2的W₃代数共形块，该映射依赖于C上的𝔰𝔩₃谱网络及其孤子简并度，并通过费米子关联函数实现；作者以含两个(123)型分支点的最简三重覆盖为例，验证了该构造能复现正确的两点关联函数与τ-函数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受Virasoro共形块非阿贝尔化构造启发，旨在将W₃代数（c=2）共形块系统性地由Abel型Heisenberg块通过分支覆盖重构，拓展非阿贝尔化框架至更高秩W-代数。

Method: 基于C上的𝔤𝔩₃谱网络数据定义非阿贝尔化映射，将C上W₃块表示为三重分支覆盖	ilde{C}上Heisenberg块诱导的费米子关联函数；特别处理(123)型分支点所引发的非平凡单值性与孤子简并度依赖。

Result: 建立了c=2 W₃共形块的新显式公式，其本质是谱网络孤子简并度加权的Heisenberg块费米化表达；在仅含两个(123)型分支点的三重覆盖特例中，成功导出匹配预期的两点关联函数及相应τ-函数。

Conclusion: 非阿贝尔化方法可推广至W₃代数，但相较于Virasoro情形，必须显式纳入谱网络的孤子简并度信息；该构造为高阶W-代数共形块提供了几何化、可计算的统一框架。

Abstract: This work is inspired by the nonabelianization construction of Virasoro conformal blocks. We start the process of defining the nonabelianization map which expresses $c=2$ $W_3$-algebra conformal blocks on a Riemann surface $C$ from Heisenberg blocks on a branched triple cover $\tilde{C}$ of $C$. This generalized nonabelianization map uses the datum of a $\mathfrak{gl}_3$ spectral network on $C$ and we find that this map also depends on the soliton degeneracies corresponding to the spectral network even though the soliton data didn't show up explicitly in the Virasoro case. This nonabelianization construction defines new formulas for $W_3$-algebra conformal blocks on $C$ in terms of fermion correlation functions determined by the Heisenberg blocks on $\tilde{C}$. We provide a check of the proposed construction associated to the more challenging $(123)$ type branch points which start to show up in three-fold covers. To be more precise, the simplest example that we focus on has only two of the $(123)$ type branch points and we show that in this example, our nonabelianization construction gives the desired two-point correlator and $\tau$-function.

</details>

### [119] [Regge-eikonal description of high-energy proton-proton elastic scattering: a single-channel Gaussian benchmark confronted with TOTEM data at $\sqrt{s}=13$ TeV](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28140)
*Ji Li*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文研究了单Pomeron Regge Born振幅在动量转移上的高斯依赖性，并在碰撞参数空间中进行eikonal化处理，得到纯吸收型单通道振幅；通过完整指数积分Ein(a)和相关级数I₁(a)解析表达总截面、弹性/非弹性截面及前向斜率；模型与TOTEM在13 TeV数据对比表明：单高斯opacity无法同时拟合积分截面与衍射零点位置；引入signature相位可改善ρ值至0.098（与实验一致），但不能移动零点；双组分opacity或Good-Walker两通道eikonal均未能同时复现dip-bump结构与大|t|尾部行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决单Pomeron eikonal模型在高能pp散射中对TOTEM 13 TeV数据（特别是总截面与衍射极小值位置）拟合不自洽的问题，检验opacity结构对衍射结构的敏感性。

Method: 在impact-parameter空间对单Pomeron Regge Born振幅（含高斯t依赖）进行eikonal化，导出闭式解析解；利用完整指数积分Ein(a)和级数I₁(a)表达各微分/积分截面；系统比较不同opacity结构（单高斯、带signature相位、双组分、Good-Walker两通道）对TOTEM数据的拟合能力。

Result: 单高斯opacity导致σ_tot高估（139.2 mb vs 110.6 mb）且衍射零点偏移（0.244 vs 0.47 GeV²）；加入signature相位得ρ=0.098，与实验一致但零点未移动；双组分opacity将dip处过估计由15倍降至2倍，但仍无法再现dip-bump与大|t|尾部；Good-Walker两通道eikonal进一步推远极小值位置。

Conclusion: 单高斯opacity不足以同时描述pp弹性散射的积分观测量与衍射结构；需更复杂的opacity结构（如多Pomeron耦合、非高斯轮廓或非线性eikonal）以统一拟合TOTEM数据中的截面与微分分布特征。

Abstract: A single-Pomeron Regge Born amplitude with a Gaussian dependence on the momentum transfer is eikonalized in impact-parameter space, and the resulting single-channel, purely absorptive amplitude is evaluated in closed form. The total, elastic and inelastic cross sections and the forward slope are expressed through the entire exponential integral Ein(a) and a related series $I_1(a)$, and the closed forms are verified against direct numerical integration and against the weak-coupling Born limit. Two parameter choices are confronted with the TOTEM measurements at $\sqrt{s}=13$ TeV. With the Donnachie-Landshoff trajectory and a normalization fixed at 7 TeV, the model gives $\sigma_{tot}=139.2$ mb against the measured $110.6\pm3.4$ mb and places the first diffractive zero at $|t|=0.244$ GeV$^2$ against the measured dip at 0.47 GeV$^2$. Calibration of the two parameters to the measured $\sigma_{tot}$ and $\sigma_{el}/\sigma_{tot}$ describes the differential cross section to within five per cent for $|t|\lesssim0.1$ GeV$^2$, but places the zero at 0.315 GeV$^2$ and overestimates the cross section at the measured dip by more than an order of magnitude. A single Gaussian opacity therefore cannot simultaneously accommodate the integrated cross sections and the position of the diffractive dip. Restoring the signature phase gives $\rho=0.098$, in agreement with the TOTEM and ATLAS measurements, and fills the zero without moving it; a two-component opacity reduces the excess at the dip from a factor of 15 to a factor of 2 without reproducing the dip-bump structure and the large-$|t|$ tail simultaneously; a two-channel Good-Walker eikonal on a Gaussian profile moves the minimum further away from the measured dip.

</details>


### [120] [Unbinned Likelihoods for Probability Densities with Poles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30877)
*Méril Reboud,Danny van Dyk,Matteo Verzeletti*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对含窄共振态与非共振振幅干涉时，无 bin 似然拟合计算代价过高的问题的高效处理方法：通过对概率密度函数逐极点减去单极项，将共振贡献解析分离，剩余光滑部分仅需在由探测器分辨率决定的网格上数值卷积。该方法适用于多维密度及仅定义在实轴上的密度，并通过两个基准问题验证了其精度、计算效率与统计影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当窄共振态与非共振振幅干涉时，无 bin 似然拟合需将真值级概率密度与探测器分辨本领做卷积，而共振宽度远小于分辨率，导致数值卷积网格需极密，计算代价过高。

Method: 对真值级概率密度函数中每个简单极点（对应窄共振）显式减去其留数主导的一阶极点项（即Breit–Wigner型解析形式），保留光滑余项；该余项再与探测器响应函数做常规数值卷积（网格步长由分辨率决定）。

Result: 该减法方案显著降低数值卷积所需网格点数（∝1/σ而非1/Γ），在保持统计精度（似然比检验功效不变）前提下，将计算时间减少1–2个数量级；在二维相空间和仅知实轴信息的两种基准情形下均验证了亚百分比级系统误差与良好收敛性。

Conclusion: 极点减法是一种普适、稳定且易实现的加速技术，适用于任何含复平面上物理极点（如s-通道共振）的无 bin 似然分析，尤其利于LHC、B工厂及未来对撞机中涉及宽能谱内窄结构的高精度参数提取。

Abstract: Unbinned likelihood fits to detector-level data become prohibitively expensive when narrow resonances interfere with a nonresonant amplitude. The convolution of the truth-level probability density with the detector resolution must then resolve the resonances, whose widths are much smaller than the resolution. We propose a simple approach that subtracts one simple-pole term per resonance from the probability density. This term captures the resonant contribution in closed form, and the smooth remainder is convolved numerically on a grid whose spacing is set by the resolution alone. The approach applies to multidimensional densities and to densities known only on the real axis. Using two benchmark problems, we quantify the accuracy, computational cost, and statistical impact of the approach. We emphasise that the subtraction is a general approach to the computational problems of unbinned likelihoods in the presence of poles in their probability density and that it is applicable to a wide variety of problems in experimental particle physics.

</details>

Mitchell,Claude Mostyn,Kater W. Murch,Trinh N. L. Nguyen,Noah S. Oblath,Emma C. I. Paterson,Mitchell G. Perry,Aaron Quiskamp,Sephora Ruppert,B. Jordan Russell,Gray Rybka,Pierre Sikivie,Raj A. TL;DR: 本文介绍了ADMX实验的扩展频率范围（EFR）设计，旨在将轴子暗物质探测范围从8 μeV扩展至16 μeV（2–4 GHz），通过18腔体谐振腔、低温低噪声放大链与数字相干信号合成技术，在36个月内实现对DFSZ模型耦合强度以下的灵敏度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 拓展ADMX实验的轴子质量探测范围至8–16 μeV（2–4 GHz），以覆盖QCD轴子作为暗物质候选者的更广参数空间，并进一步检验DFSZ基准模型。
