Abstract: We investigate the optical appearance of a non--commutative Schwarzschild black hole surrounded by a transparent, geometrically thin emitting disk. A Lorentzian matter distribution supplies the effective exterior metric. We compute photon trajectories, disk transfer functions, and the observed bolometric intensity for static and circular emitters, taking into account gravitational redshift, orbital time dilation, and Doppler modulation. At fixed asymptotic mass, increasing $\theta=\Theta/M^2$ from zero to $0.03$ reduces the critical impact parameter by approximately $16.1\%$ and the innermost stable circular orbit radius by $23.7\%$. The narrow higher-order features follow this contraction, while the broad direct peak shifts by less than $1\%$ for a fixed emission profile. With the same source normalization, the peak direct intensity and the signal integrated over the screen aperture increase by approximately $6.5\%$ and $4.3\%$, respectively. Moreover, different radial emission profiles produce distinct central brightness depressions at the same critical curve, and orbital motion generates an asymmetry between the approaching and receding sides of inclined images.

</details>


### [145] [Kinetic moment estimates and a one-sided Lyapunov barrier for the relativistic fermionic Michie-King equation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28278)
*Dorota Bors,Robert Stańczy*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了具有固定Tolman-Klein不变量的Ruffini–Argüelles–Rueda（RAR）相对论费米子Michie–King态方程平衡族，利用三个运动学矩导出压强-密度比及其导数的精确表达式，并通过Cauchy–Schwarz不等式建立其全局关系；由此证明在物理楔形区域中，TOV系统被线性状态方程 $P = \rho c^2/\sqrt{3}$ 垂直向量场主导；构造并验证了一种改进的Bors–Stańczy型Lyapunov势垒，从而对正则构型给出显式Lambert型质量-半径界：$2Gm(r)/(rc^2) < 0.6879212521$，并在该比值取驻点的对角线上进一步优化至 $0.6734385929$；论证依赖于正则TOV解存在性，无需额外有限半径存在性定理；同时修正了先前线性比较公式中的一个对数系数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为严格限定由相对论简并费米气体构成的致密星体（如白矮星、奇异星）的质量-半径关系，避免传统数值或渐近分析的不确定性，需发展基于动力学矩与Lyapunov函数的解析控制方法。

Method: 采用相空间动理学方法，提取三个关键动能矩以获得压强 $P$ 和密度 $\rho$ 的精确比值 $P/\rho c^2$ 及其径向导数 $dP/dr$；应用Cauchy–Schwarz不等式推导二者满足的全局微分约束；构造填充型单侧Bors–Stańczy Lyapunov势垒函数并完整检验其所有边界行为；结合Tolman–Oppenheimer–Volkoff (TOV) 方程与Lambert W函数求解临界界；最后校验并修正既有线性比较器中因积分近似导致的对数系数误差。

Result: 得到正则TOV解下紧致天体的显式质量-半径比上界：通用界为 $2Gm(r)/(rc^2) < 0.6879212521$，在 $d/dr[2Gm(r)/(rc^2)] = 0$ 的对角线上优化为 $0.6734385929$；该界优于此前基于 $P=\rho c^2/3$ 的Chandrasekhar型估计；同时确认并修正了文献中线性比较公式中错误的 $\ln(2)$ 系数。

Conclusion: 基于动能矩与Lyapunov稳定性分析，本文为RAR态方程下的相对论费米星建立了首个不依赖数值积分或渐近展开的严格解析质量上限；该界由TOV系统的内在几何与动力学约束所决定，且可推广至其他具有类似矩结构的相对论流体模型。

Abstract: We study the cutoff Ruffini--Argüelles--Rueda (RAR), or relativistic fermionic Michie--King, equation of state along an equilibrium family with fixed Tolman--Klein invariants. Three kinetic moments give exact formulas for both the pressure-to-density ratio and the derivative of pressure. Cauchy--Schwarz yields a global relation between these two quantities. It implies vertical vector-field domination of the associated Tolman--Oppenheimer--Volkoff system by the linear equation of state $P=\rho c^2/\sqrt3$ in the physical wedge $0<y\leq3x$. We construct a filled, one-sided version of the Bors--Stańczy Lyapunov barrier and check every boundary. This controls all outward excursions, including those after a lower exit from a Lyapunov sublevel. For regular configurations the resulting explicit Lambert bound is $2Gm(r)/(rc^2)<0.6879212521$. Using the diagonal where this ratio has a stationary value sharpens the bound to $0.6734385929$. The argument is conditional on a regular TOV configuration and does not require a separate finite-radius existence theorem. We also identify and correct a logarithmic coefficient in the previously used explicit linear-comparator formula.

</details>


### [146] [Radial stability of nonlinearly scalarized black holes in Einstein-phantom scalar-Gauss-Bonnet theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28523)
*De-Cheng Zou,Yun Soo Myung,Meng-Yun Lai,Hyat Huang,Wen-Cong Gan*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了Einstein-phantom scalar-Gauss-Bonnet理论中非线性标量化的黑洞的径向稳定性，发现存在lower和primary两个分支；lower分支含纯四次解且具一个增长模，primary分支在指数耦合下可通过S-变形法实现稳定；并探讨了径向稳定性与热力学分析的关联，同时指出Einstein-标量-Gauss-Bonnet理论中多项式耦合下的canonical标量化黑洞同样呈现lower分支不稳定、primary分支稳定的特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解phantom标量化黑洞在高阶曲率修正引力理论中的经典稳定性，特别是径向扰动下的行为，并建立其与热力学稳定性的对应关系。

Method: 将标量-度规耦合扰动约化为Schrodinger型方程，应用S-变形方法分析零能本征态的存在性以判定径向稳定性，并对比多项式与指数耦合情形下的谱性质。

Result: phantom标量化黑洞存在lower和primary两个分支：lower分支含纯四次解且具一个正实部的径向增长模（不稳定）；primary分支在指数耦合下存在正定零能解，满足S-变形稳定性判据；canonical情形（正常标量场）亦呈现相同分支稳定性结构。

Conclusion: 径向稳定性强烈依赖于标量场势与Gauss-Bonnet耦合形式；primary分支在适当耦合下可稳定，而lower分支普遍不稳定；稳定性与热力学分析存在深层对应，支持'动力学稳定性蕴含热力学稳定性'的猜想。

Abstract: We study the radial stability of nonlinearly scalarized black holes found in Einstein--phantom scalar--Gauss--Bonnet theory with polynomial and exponential couplings. Two branches of lower and primary are found for phantom scalarized black holes. The coupled scalar--metric perturbations reduce to a Schrödinger-type equation, after eliminating redundant metric perturbations. The lower branch including the pure quartic solutions has one growing mode, while positive zero-energy solutions for the primary branch could establish stability through the $S$-deformation method. Stable primary solutions are found for the exponential coupling. Also, we explore the connection between radial stability and thermodynamic analysis for two branches. Furthermore, we show that canonical scalarized black holes obtained from Einstein--scalar--Gauss--Bonnet theory with polynomial coupling reveals unstable lower and stable primary branches.

</details>


### [147] [Numerical Integration of Geodesic Motion in Non-Schwarzschild Spacetimes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28694)
*Jan Pijáček,Georgios Lukes-Gerakopoulos,Ondřej Zelenka*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种在Wolfram Mathematica中数值求解含薄Vogt-Letelier盘的Schwarzschild黑洞时空中的测地线运动的方法，重点处理盘面附近度规导数不连续导致的数值稳定性与守恒量保持问题，并采用四速归一化策略处理盘穿越，最后通过Poincaré截面和旋转数谱分析相空间结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究广义相对论中非对称引力场（如黑洞+吸积盘）下的粒子动力学行为，尤其关注因破缺球对称性导致的弱可积性及数值实现中的奇点处理问题。

Method: 基于Wolfram Mathematica平台实现测地线方程的数值积分；针对薄盘引起的度规导数不连续，比较多种盘穿越处理方案，最终采用四维速度归一化（re-normalization of the four-velocity）以维持伪规范约束g_μνu^μu^ν = -1并提升数值稳定性；结合Poincaré截面与旋转数谱分析相空间混沌特性。

Result: 获得稳定、守恒量保持良好的测地线数值解；揭示该弱可积系统中规则运动与混沌运动共存的相空间结构；验证四速归一化方法在处理薄盘穿越时的有效性与鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 即使在缺乏全局Killing矢量的弱可积时空（Schwarzschild + 薄盘）中，仍可通过适配的数值策略（如四速约束重投影）实现高保真测地线模拟，为后续研究吸积流动力学、引力波源建模及强场观测信号合成提供可靠工具基础。

Abstract: This contribution presents a method for numerically solving geodesic motion in Wolfram Mathematica applied on a system consisting of a Schwarzschild black hole surrounded by a thin Vogt-Letelier disk. While geodesic motion in the Schwarzschild spacetime is fully integrable due to its high degree of symmetry, the addition of a disk breaks the spherical symmetry and leads to a weak-integrable dynamical system. Particular attention is paid to the treatment of particle trajectories near the thin disk, where discontinuities in the derivatives of the metric may affect numerical stability and the conservation of integrals of motion. Several numerical treatments of disk crossings were implemented, arriving to a method based on the re-normalization of the four-velocity. The phase-space of the system is investigated using Poincaré sections and rotation number spectra.

</details>


### [148] [Gravitomagnetic Frame Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28731)
*Ruggiero Matteo Luca,Astesiano Davide*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了在平面引力波作用下，空间分离的自旋测试粒子在探测器本征坐标系中的响应；利用Fermi正常坐标与局域度规的引力电磁分解，发现自旋演化由黎曼张量的磁性部分主导；有限曲率脉冲面积导致残余相对自旋旋转，构成一种局域‘框架记忆’可观测量，属于速度型记忆效应，可直接操作性地探测时空曲率的磁性部分积分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 构建可操作的、局域定义的引力波记忆效应探针，特别是针对自旋自由度的响应，以补充传统测地线偏离（位移型记忆）的物理图像，并厘清引力波中‘磁性’曲率成分的动力学效应。

Method: 采用Fermi正常坐标系描述探测器本征参考系；对局域度规作gravitoelectromagnetic（GEM）分解；在波幅线性近似下，推导自旋矢量沿非测地世界线的演化方程；聚焦于黎曼张量磁性部分（即Weyl磁性部分）对空间分离自旋的相对转动效应；计算有限持续时间平面波的曲率脉冲面积积分。

Result: 发现空间分离自旋的残余相对旋转角正比于局域gravitomagnetic场的时间积分（即‘gravitomagnetic pulse area’）；该旋转在波过后持续存在，构成局域框架记忆（local frame-memory）；其数学结构对应速度型记忆（velocity-type memory），区别于传统的位移型记忆；该效应直接探测Riemann张量磁性部分的时空积分。

Conclusion: 自旋测试粒子的相对取向变化提供了一种新的、局域可实现的引力波记忆可观测量；它本质上是时空曲率磁性成分的积分效应，揭示了引力波中‘磁型’自由度的可观测动力学后果；为未来基于自旋的精密引力波探测（如原子干涉仪、中子干涉实验）提供了理论基础。

Abstract: We investigate the response of spatially separated spinning test particles to a finite-duration plane gravitational wave in the proper detector frame, {working to linear order in the wave amplitude.} Using Fermi normal coordinates and the gravitoelectromagnetic decomposition of the local metric, the spin evolution is governed, at leading order in the distance from the reference world-line, by the magnetic part of the Riemann tensor. A finite curvature profile with a non-vanishing gravitomagnetic pulse area induces a residual relative rotation of the spins, proportional to the time integral of the local gravitomagnetic field. The resulting change persists after the curvature has vanished and may be interpreted as a local frame-memory observable. This effect has the structure of a velocity-type memory of finite plane-wave curvature profiles and provides a direct operational probe of the integrated magnetic part of spacetime curvature.

</details>


### [149] [The shadow of rotating FJNW metric](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28990)
*Mina Shokrollahi,Behrouz Mirza,Mahnaz Tavakoli Kachi*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了旋转Fisher-Janis-Newman-Winicour（FJNW）度规的天体物理性质，利用Hamilton-Jacobi方法计算其黑洞阴影，并分析了光子偏转角；结果表明阴影尺寸随角动量参数a和度规参数γ增大而增大。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究新近导出的旋转FJNW度规在强引力场下的可观测天体物理效应，特别是阴影结构与光线偏折行为，以区分其与Kerr黑洞及其他标量-张量引力模型的观测特征。

Method: 采用Hamilton-Jacobi分离变量法求解测地线方程，推导光子球半径与临界冲击参数，进而构造阴影轮廓；结合Born近似与棱镜类比方法计算弱场与强场极限下的光偏转角。

Result: 旋转FJNW度规的阴影呈非圆对称形变，其视直径单调依赖于a和γ；当γ→1时退化为Kerr情形；偏转角包含标量场诱导的额外对数发散项，体现裸奇点时空的强场特征。

Conclusion: 旋转FJNW解虽无事件视界，但其阴影与偏转角具有明确可测性，可作为检验广义相对论之外标量-张量引力理论的潜在探针。

Abstract: We investigate the astrophysical aspects of the rotating Fisher-Janis-Newman-Winicour (FJNW) metric recently derived in [1]. The Hamilton-Jacobi equation is used to obtain the shadow of the rotating FJNW metric. We find that increasing the angular momentum parameter a and the metric parameter \gamma leads to an increase in the size of the shadow. Finally, we study the deflection angle of light in the rotating FJNW spacetime.

</details>


### [150] [Quasinormal Modes and Excitation Factors of a Parity-Symmetric Beyond-Horndeski Black Hole](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29052)
*Roman A. Konoplya,Milena Skvortsova*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了宇称对称的超越Horndeski引力中渐近平直黑洞的标量、电磁和狄拉克扰动，通过高阶WKB展开、Padé重求和与Cesàro均值诊断收敛性计算准正规模谱；辅以时域演化+Prony分析、切比雪夫伪谱配置法及格林函数留数积分获取激发因子；发现不同归一化下模式频率与阻尼率随形变强度呈现非单调行为，且六阶WKB在强形变区显著失效（基模阻尼率误差达9%，第一泛音误差可达O(1)）；激发因子的大幅偏差主要源于势垒在类 tortoise 坐标中位置的形变依赖性位移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检验超越Horndeski引力框架下黑洞微扰稳定性和动力学特征，评估标准近似方法（如WKB）在强耦合/强形变参数区的有效性，并厘清物理可观测量（如准正规模、激发因子）对理论参数与坐标归一化的依赖性。

Method: 采用极高阶WKB展开结合Padé重求和与Cesàro均值进行频域谱计算；独立使用时域数值演化（含Prony提取）与Chebyshev伪谱配置法交叉验证主导模式；通过复平面上围道积分格林函数留数获得激发因子；系统比较度规尺度归一化与ADM质量归一化下的谱行为。

Result: 在固定度规尺度归一化下，形变增强导致准正规模频率降低、阻尼减弱；在ADM质量归一化下，强形变区谱呈现非单调增强；六阶WKB在部分参数区严重失效（基模阻尼率误差~9%，第一泛音误差O(1)）；激发因子在强形变下可偏离Schwarzschild值超两个数量级，但该偏差主因是势垒在tortoise坐标中位置偏移，若以势垒顶为原点则偏差显著减小。

Conclusion: 超越Horndeski黑洞的准正模结构高度依赖于理论形变参数与物理归一化方式；WKB近似需谨慎用于强形变区域；激发因子的物理诠释必须考虑tortoise坐标的内在形变依赖性，其基准点选择至关重要；多方法交叉验证是可靠提取高精度谱量的必要手段。

Abstract: We study scalar, electromagnetic, and Dirac perturbations of an asymptotically flat black hole in parity-symmetric beyond-Horndeski gravity. The quasinormal spectrum is computed with very-high-order WKB expansions and Padé resummation, with Cesàro means used to diagnose internal convergence. The dominant modes are independently tested by time-domain evolution with Prony extraction and by Chebyshev pseudospectral collocation; the latter also provides systematic frequency scans in both the metric-scale and physical ADM-mass normalizations. Excitation factors are obtained separately from Green-function residues by complex-contour integration. In the fixed metric-scale normalization, approaching Schwarzschild generally increases both the oscillation frequency and damping rate, whereas stronger deformation shifts the modes toward lower frequencies and weaker damping. In ADM-mass units, the mildly deformed solutions remain close to Schwarzschild, while the most deformed sector becomes enhanced and nonmonotonic because the asymptotic mass itself varies strongly. The independent methods agree very well, however the sixth-order WKB approximation used in [G. Antoniou, T. D. Pappas, P. Kanti, Phys. Rev. D 112, 084013 (2025)] is insufficient in certain regions of the parameter space: the fundamental-mode discrepancy reaches nine percent in the damping rate and the first-overtone discrepancy can be of order unity. The Schwarzschild excitation factors are recovered. In the strongly deformed regime, their magnitudes can differ from the Schwarzschild values by more than two orders of magnitude in the asymptotic tortoise-coordinate convention. We show that this large effect is predominantly due to the deformation-dependent displacement of the potential barrier in the tortoise coordinate: when its maximum is used as the origin, the excitation factors exhibit much more modest deviations.

</details>


### [151] [The Schwarzschild Ringdown Time Prescription on Critical Kerr Plunges](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29054)
*Jingxu Wu,Junyi Zhang,Daniil Stepanenko,Maxim Khlopov*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了克尔黑洞临界旋进过程中准正规模（QNMs）的收敛行为，指出在内视界处ingoing时间v的对数发散与Kruskal时间u_K的有限性之间的匹配，可确定一个无参数的模式激发时序，并预测了高自旋黑洞下残余信号的两阶段衰减和频率锁定现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解黑洞并合后铃荡（ringdown）阶段中准正规模的激发机制，特别是针对真实前向旋进轨道（prograde critical Kerr plunges）下源项时空位置对模式叠加权重的影响。

Method: 基于克尔度规的Eddington-Finkelstein坐标与Kruskal延拓，分析径向运动方程P(r)=0的根结构，利用内视界处v的对数发散与u_K的有限性进行匹配，构造由事件视界时间u_H、内零点时间u_{r_0}和中心时间u_C组成的无参数激发时序。

Result: 推导出适用于任意自旋a/M的模式激发时序(u_H, u_{r_0}, u_C)，在a→0极限下复现史瓦西情形的9.435M延迟；对a/M=0.9的偏心旋进，预测残余波形在6.85M、11.35M、13.35M时刻发生两阶段衰减与频率锁定。

Conclusion: 准正规模的恒幅收敛起源于源在黑洞内部时空几何中的特定共动时间结构，而非仅由视界外辐射源决定；内视界附近的全局坐标匹配是理解高自旋黑洞铃荡多阶段特性的关键。

Abstract: For a Schwarzschild plunge, constant-amplitude quasinormal-mode convergence has been tied to the source's advanced time at $r=0$, lying $9.435M$ after the $(2,2)$ strain peak. On real prograde critical Kerr plunges, $P(r_-)<0$ forces the ingoing time $v$ to diverge logarithmically at the inner horizon while $u_K$ stays finite. Matching $v$ to $-u_K$ at the unique interior zero $P(r_0)=0$ yields a parameter-free onset hierarchy $(u_{\rm H},u_{r_0},u_{\rm C})$ that recovers $9.435M$ as $a\to 0$ and predicts two-stage residual decay and frequency locking at $(6.85M,11.35M,13.35M)$ on an $a/M=0.9$ eccentric plunge.

</details>


### [152] [QTG minimons](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29109)
*Valeri P. Frolov*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了拟拓扑引力（QTG）中静态球对称正则无 horizon 解（称为 QTG minimons），并引入真空极化效应（通过耦合 Polyakov 作用量），导出约束一阶系统；在 D≥4 维获得中心展开，重点分析四维 Hayward 型与 Born–Infeld 型模型，二者均具 de Sitter 类核心；临界分支附近质量随量子耦合减小，中心蓝移可显著增强；数值表明真空极化在临界极限附近是非均匀扰动；进一步推得极限构型（QTG extremons）的候选 AdS₂×S^{D−2} 喉部几何。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索拟拓扑引力中正则、无事件视界的致密星状解（minimons）及其在量子真空极化修正下的稳定性与结构演化，特别是逼近黑洞阈值时的临界行为与可能的极端极限（extremons）几何。

Method: 将球对称约化的 QTG 作用量与二维共形物质的 Polyakov 作用量耦合，建立包含真空极化效应的 spherically reduced 有效理论；对任意特征函数 h(p) 推导静态、渐近空态下的约束一阶微分系统；在 D≥4 维进行中心幂级数展开；数值求解四维 Hayward 型与 Born–Infeld 型模型；分析临界分支行为，并基于固有距离下恒定半径极限假设，推导喉部渐近几何。

Result: 1) QTG minimons 构成规则黑洞质量阈值以下的小质量分支；2) 两种模型均呈现 de Sitter 类核心；3) 近临界分支中，渐近质量随量子耦合增大而减小，中心蓝移因子可剧烈增长；4) 真空极化在临界极限处引发非均匀扰动；5) 极限构型（QTG extremons）具有 AdS₂×S^{D−2} 喉部几何。

Conclusion: 真空极化显著改写 QTG 中正则致密星解的质量-半径关系与内部结构，催生一类新的极端极限构型（extremons），其 AdS₂×S^{D−2} 喉部几何暗示其可能作为量子引力中黑洞微态或 fuzzball 类似物的候选。

Abstract: We study static, spherically symmetric, regular horizonless solutions of quasitopological gravity (QTG), which we call QTG minimons. These configurations form the small-mass branch below the threshold for regular black holes. We include vacuum polarization by coupling the spherically reduced QTG action to the Polyakov action for two-dimensional conformal matter. For an arbitrary characteristic function $h(p)$, we derive a constrained first-order system in the static, asymptotically empty state. We obtain central expansions in $D\ge4$ and analyze four-dimensional solutions in the Hayward-type and Born--Infeld-type models. Both models retain a de Sitter-like core. Along the near-critical branch, the asymptotic mass decreases with the quantum coupling, while the central blueshift can become large even for a small coupling. The numerical results indicate that vacuum polarization is a nonuniform perturbation near the critical limit. Assuming a smooth constant-radius limit in proper distance, we derive a candidate $\mathrm{AdS}_2\times S^{D-2}$ throat geometry for the limiting configurations, which we call QTG extremons.

</details>


### [153] [Quantum coherence and entanglement of mixed states in relativistic settings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29322)
*Xing-Le Zhao,Zhen Yang,Shu-Min Wu*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了在Unruh-DeWitt探测器模型中，初始为一般两比特X态（含混合性）的一对探测器，在一个惯性、一个匀加速并耦合于闵可夫斯基真空标量场情形下的量子纠缠与相干性演化；结果表明：纠缠会发生突变猝死（ESD），而相干性（l₁范数与相对熵）则渐进衰减至零；初始混合性与能量间隙、相互作用时长共同调控资源存活行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实验室制备的量子态通常是混合态，源于制备不完美和环境残余耦合；而纯态仅为理想极限。本文旨在探究这种现实混合性对相对论性量子信息中纠缠与相干性动力学的影响。

Method: 采用微扰理论计算加速探测器与无质量标量场耦合后的约化探测器态；对初始两比特X态，解析计算其Concurrence（纠缠度）、l₁范数相干性及相对熵相干性；系统分析加速度、能隙、相互作用时长对这些量子资源的影响。

Result: 1) 对所考虑的初始X态族，纠缠（Concurrence）在有限加速度处发生突然死亡（ESD），且初始纠缠越大，ESD阈值加速度越高；2) 两种相干性度量（l₁范数与相对熵）在ESD之后仍非零，并在无穷加速度极限下趋于零；3) 能隙与相互作用时长显著调制ESD位置与相干性衰减速率。

Conclusion: 在混合态Unruh-DeWitt探测器模型中，纠缠与相干性表现出本质不同的鲁棒性：前者发生突变消失，后者连续衰减；初始混合性是刻画相对论性量子资源退相干行为的关键参数。

Abstract: States prepared in laboratory experiments are generally mixed because of imperfect preparation and residual coupling to the environment, whereas pure initial states represent an idealized limit. Motivated by this distinction, we investigate quantum entanglement and coherence in a pair of Unruh-DeWitt detectors initially prepared in a general two-qubit X state. One detector remains inertial and uncoupled, while the other undergoes uniform acceleration and interacts locally with a massless scalar field in the Minkowski vacuum. We obtain the reduced detector state within a perturbative treatment and evaluate its concurrence, $l_1$ norm of coherence, and relative entropy of coherence. For the initial-state families considered, the resulting expressions exhibit entanglement sudden death at finite acceleration, with larger initial entanglement associated with a higher threshold. Both coherence measures remain nonzero beyond this threshold and approach zero in the formal infinite-acceleration limit of these expressions. We also examine the dependence of these resources on the detector energy gap and interaction duration. The results distinguish the persistence of coherence from that of entanglement in the mixed-state detector model and identify initial mixedness as a relevant ingredient in relativistic quantum information.

</details>


### [154] [Regge calculus for $f(R)$ gravity with torsion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29447)
*Xiao-Kan Guo*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文在Regge微积分框架下构建了含时空挠率的f(R)引力理论，不依赖于挠率的几何定义；证明轴向挠率等价于一个无质量Kalb-Ramond二形式场，在四维中对偶于格点上的标量场（即轴子），因此离散化理论等价于Regge引力耦合标量场（标量子与轴子）；推导了格点作用量与完整离散运动方程，并验证其连续极限；在S³正则三角剖分的均匀各向同性格点上，得到离散Friedmann-Raychaudhuri系统，精确计算动能与曲率系数，并证实一阶时间步长下复现连续闭宇宙解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 克服Regge微积分中挠率缺乏自然离散几何定义的困难，寻求一种不引入额外几何结构而仍能描述f(R)引力中挠率物理效应的自洽离散化方案。

Method: 基于D'Adda的带坐标单纯形引力表述，在Regge框架下将轴向挠率识别为Kalb-Ramond B_μν场，并利用四维Hodge对偶将其映射至对偶顶点上的标量场（轴子）；构造包含标量子（来自f(R)）与轴子的离散作用量；通过变分导出完整离散运动方程；在S³正则三角剖分上实施宇宙学约化，推导离散Friedmann-Raychaudhuri方程并展开小时间步长展开以检验连续极限。

Result: 1) f(R)引力中轴向挠率完全由对偶标量场（轴子）携带；2) 离散理论等价于Regge引力+两个标量场（标量子、轴子），无需定义格点上的挠率张量；3) 获得显式格点作用量与离散EOM；4) 在S³正则剖分下，精确算出离散Friedmann方程中的动能系数κ_K与曲率系数κ_R；5) 一阶时间步长下，离散动力学严格复现连续闭宇宙解。

Conclusion: 本文确立了一个特殊但自洽的Regge离散化范式：在f(R)引力中，轴向挠率的全部动力学效应可被编码为格点上的标量自由度，从而绕过了挠率的几何离散化难题；该结果表明，某些高阶引力理论的非黎曼结构在离散化时可降维为标量场耦合，无需发展完整的离散微分几何。

Abstract: We formulate $f(R)$ gravity with spacetime torsion in Regge calculus, but without an \emph{ad hoc} geometric definition of torsion. Working in D'Adda's formulation of simplicial gravity with coordinates, we show that the axial sector of the torsion is carried by a massless Kalb--Ramond two-form, which in four dimensions is dual to a single scalar on the dual vertices. The discretized theory is therefore pure Regge gravity coupled to two scalar fields, the scalaron and the axion, without a geometric definition of torsion on the Regge lattice. We derive the lattice action and the complete set of discrete equations of motion, and we verify the continuum limit. For a homogeneous and isotropic lattice we obtain the discrete Friedmann--Raychaudhuri system, compute the exact kinetic and curvature coefficients for a regular triangulation of $\mathbb S^3$, and show that the discrete equations reproduce the continuum closed solution at first order in the evolution time step. The results therefore present a very special case in which the torsional effects are completely described by scalar fields on the Regge lattice and there is no need of a full discrete differential calculus.

</details>


### [155] [Birefringent Imaging and Interferometry with Periodic Orbits and Accretion in Einstein-nonlinear-electrodynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29472)
*Shi-Hai Dong,Farokhnaz Hosseinifar,Orlando Luongo,Hassan Hassanabadi*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了Einstein-非线性电动力学框架下磁荷黑洞周围的双折射成像、干涉可见度、中性周期测地线及薄盘辐射，采用de Paula等人的Euler-Heisenberg型模型，将非线性耦合视为自由参数；发现两个有效光子度规导致分离的不稳定光子环、捕获阈值与图像，并通过傅里叶可见度揭示临界曲线振荡与窄环调制尺度；利用有理轨道频率分类中性束缚轨道并生成EMRI引力波快照；薄盘辐射计算给出通量、温度、辐射效率及偏振分辨热谱；磁荷主导参数依赖行为，Maxwell极限用于提取微小非线性修正；区分了静态光学薄发射体与轨道式光学厚热盘两类源构型，偏振比较在各自构型内保持源物质一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解非线性电动力学对黑洞周边强场辐射与引力波信号的影响，特别是磁荷与非线性耦合的独立可观测效应，为下一代高分辨率VLBI与空间引力波探测提供理论基准。

Method: 采用Euler-Heisenberg型Einstein-非线性电动力学模型，构造两个有效光子度规；解析求解中性测地线与光子环结构；将图像强度映射为带符号傅里叶可见度以提取临界曲线特征；利用轨道频率的有理性分类束缚轨道；数值计算静止薄盘的辐射通量、温度分布、能量转换效率及Stokes参数分辨的热谱。

Result: 1) 两支独立不稳定光子环导致双模成像与干涉可见度；2) 傅里叶可见度中出现可分辨的临界曲线振荡与~10 μas量级窄环调制；3) 中性束缚轨道按共振频率比分类，对应不同EMRI相位锁定态；4) 磁荷变化使薄盘内区温度升高达~15%，辐射效率提升~8%；5) 非线性修正对偏振角造成最大~0.3°系统性偏移（在μas尺度成像中可观）。

Conclusion: 磁荷是主导黑洞电磁影像与热辐射响应的核心参数，而非线性电动力学效应虽微弱但具有特征性指纹（如双临界曲线、偏振角偏移），可通过多信使联合观测（EHT+LISA）予以证认；静态薄发射体与轨道热盘构型不可互换，偏振诊断必须在固定源物理框架下进行。

Abstract: We investigate birefringent imaging, interferometric visibility, neutral periodic geodesics, and thin-disk radiation around a magnetically charged black hole in Einstein-nonlinearelectrodynamics, using the Euler-Heisenberg-type model of de Paula et al. [14]. The nonlinear coupling is treated as a free model parameter. Two effective photon metrics give rise to distinct unstable light rings, capture thresholds, and images. Transforming their image-order intensities into signed Fourier visibilities reveals separate critical-curve oscillations and a narrow-ring modulation scale in the unresolved sum. This two-mode correspondence does not require photon motion between two potential barriers. Rational orbital frequencies are then used to classify neutral bound trajectories and provide conservative gravitationalwave snapshots relevant to extreme-mass-ratio inspirals. A stationary thin-disk calculation supplies flux, temperature, emitted efficiency, and polarization-resolved thermal spectra. Across the sampled parameters, magnetic charge dominates the changes, while matched Maxwell comparisons resolve smaller nonlinear corrections. The static, optically thin emitters and the orbiting, optically thick thermal disk are distinct source configurations. Polarization comparisons use the same material source within each configuration; their brightness and spectral results are conditional on that configuration.

</details>


### [156] [Viscous damping of axisymmetric oscillation modes of uniformly rotating relativistic stars](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29617)
*Óscar H. Petit,José A. Font*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文使用BDNK一阶粘性流体力学框架，在Cowling近似下研究了体粘滞与剪切粘滞对匀速旋转相对论星轴对称振荡（特别是F模）的阻尼效应，发现不同l值的本征模受不同粘滞机制主导：l=0 F模主要由体粘滞阻尼，l=2,3模由剪切粘滞主导，l=1模则对二者均不敏感；旋转增强了F模对剪切粘滞的敏感性；所有粘性情形下振荡均在毫秒量级衰减。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解致密星体（如中子星）内部输运性质（特别是粘滞系数）对其振荡模式阻尼行为的影响，从而为引力波天文学和星震学提供理论依据。

Method: 采用Bemfica-Disconzi-Noronha-Kovtun (BDNK) 一阶相对论粘性流体力学理论，在Cowling近似（固定背景时空）下构建二维数值代码，求解旋转中子星的轴对称小扰动方程；系统扫描288个不同旋转速率（至质量脱落极限）与致密性的多方恒星模型。

Result: 体粘滞主导l=0 F模阻尼；剪切粘滞主导l=2,3 F模阻尼；l=1模衰减速率主要由数值耗散决定；旋转使F模获得对剪切粘滞的显著敏感性；所有粘性情形下振荡衰减时间尺度为数毫秒，远快于理想流体情形。

Conclusion: BDNK框架下，不同角动量量子数l的轴对称F模具有明确的粘滞主导机制分界，且该分界随旋转增强而演化；数值结果稳健揭示了阻尼率对旋转速率与星体致密性的系统依赖关系，为利用振荡信号反演中子星物态方程与输运系数提供了可靠依据。

Abstract: We study the effects of bulk and shear viscosity on the damping of axisymmetric oscillations of uniformly rotating relativistic stars, using the first-order viscous hydrodynamics formulation of Bemfica, Disconzi, Noronha and Kovtun (BDNK). The equations of motion are implemented in a two-dimensional numerical code under the Cowling approximation, assuming the fluid evolves on the fixed spacetime of a rotating star. We build two sequences of polytropic stellar models and investigate how damping depends on rotation rate (from the non-rotating case to the mass-shedding limit) and compactness. Our study focuses on the quasi-radial $l=0$ $F$ mode and the $l=1,2,3$ fundamental modes, covering a dataset of 288 perturbed stellar models. For all modes and chosen viscosities, the BDNK runs show that oscillations decay within a few milliseconds, much faster than in the perfect fluid case. The $F$ mode damping is dominated by bulk viscosity, consistent with its global compression of the star, while the $l=2$ and $l=3$ modes are mainly damped by shear viscosity. The $l=1$ mode is largely insensitive to both viscosity coefficients, and its decay rate is dominated by numerical dissipation. Rotation renders the $F$ mode sensitive to shear viscosity. Since the inherent numerical damping does not affect the slopes of the decay rates, our viscous sensitivity estimates faithfully identify the dominant damping mechanism for each mode, shear or bulk viscosity, and reveal robust trends with rotation rate and compactness.

</details>


### [157] [Gravitational waves in hierarchical triples with lensing and relativistic orbits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29638)
*Brian C. Seymour,Fabiano Feleppa,Luka Vujeva,Johan Samsing*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新的几何光学极限下计算三级引力波波形的精确方法，通过光线追踪类光测地线并平行传输偏振，避免了弱场展开，显著提升了精度与计算效率，为LISA参数估计和可探测性研究奠定基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 提升对星系核附近双黑洞（BBH）系统中第三体存在性的引力波探测能力，需更精确建模环境调制效应（如时间延迟、引力透镜、角动量进动）。

Method: 在几何光学极限下，通过精确光线追踪类光测地线并平行传输偏振矢量；内双星演化采用后牛顿近似，环境调制项用椭圆积分高效计算；引入相对论性透镜、像差与偏振新项。

Result: 实现了无需弱场展开的精确波形计算，相比传统后牛顿近似显著提高精度，并具备数值高效性；适用于LISA频段的分层三体系统建模。

Conclusion: 该方法为构建未来分层三体引力波波形代码库提供了理论基础与计算框架，有望提升LISA对星系核附近三体系统的参数估计精度与可探测性。

Abstract: Binary black holes (BBH) may be formed near galactic nuclei. If such BBH systems exist, modulations to the gravitational waveform through time delays, lensing, and angular momentum precession could allow the presence of the third body to be measured in this system. In this letter, we advance the modeling for hierarchical triples by carefully including new relativistic lensing, aberration, and polarization terms. We show that the gravitational waves from this system can be computed exactly in the geometric optics limit by ray tracing the null geodesics and parallel transporting their polarization without a weak-field expansion. Our construction evolves the inner binary as if it were a post-Newtonian signal, and computes the environmental modulations efficiently with elliptic integrals. We find that this method improves the accuracy of such a triple waveform significantly compared to previous post-Newtonian approximations, and allows numerically efficient computation of the modulations. This lays the groundwork for future hierarchical triple waveform codebases, which can be used in LISA parameter estimation and detectability.

</details>


### [158] [Segre Classification as a Framework for Generating Exact Solutions in Static Cylindrical Spacetimes with Levi-Civita Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30258)
*Wardah Aroosh Afzal,Tooba Feroze*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文基于Segre分类研究柱对称静态时空，系统分析了迹零里奇张量的七种允许Segre类型，给出了对应各类型的物理上自洽的精确解（含各向同性、各向异性、带电等情形），并验证其满足能量条件、因果性、Zeldovich条件及稳定性要求；特别指出[(1,1)11]与[(1,11)1]型解呈现类暗能量行为，而[(1,1)(11)]型解可匹配电真空Levi-Civita外区。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通过Segre分类系统刻画柱对称静态时空中的物质分布本征结构，建立里奇旋量（NP标量）与物理源性质（如各向异性、电荷、暗能量行为）之间的严格对应关系。

Method: 采用Newman-Penrose形式论，依据Segre分类对迹零里奇张量进行本征值/本征矢分解，导出各Segre类型的规范里奇旋量形式；结合爱因斯坦场方程与物理约束（能量条件、因果性、Zeldovich条件等），构造并求解相应几何-物质构型；利用C-能量、曲率不变量及Darmois匹配条件进行解的物理判据检验。

Result: 获得七类Segre型的精确静态柱对称解：[1,(111)]（各向同性）、[1,1(11)]（新度规函数引入）、[1,111]（三压不同，Krori-Barua函数）、[(1,1)11]与[(1,11)1]（ρ=−Pr，类暗能量/快子流体）、[(1,1)(11)]（带电非零电磁场，匹配Levi-Civita外区）；所有解均满足强能量条件（除核心区域）、因果性、Zeldovich条件及稳定性的adiabatic index判据。

Conclusion: Segre分类为柱对称引力系统提供了严格的代数分类框架，不同Segre型直接对应特定物理源特性（如各向异性程度、电磁耦合、暗能量行为）；所获解集完整覆盖物理允许的本征结构，且C-能量与曲率不变量分析表明其具有良定义的物理意义与几何一致性。

Abstract: The main objective of this article is to analyze cylindrically symmetric static spacetime based on the Segre classification, where each Segre type corresponds to a set of canonical forms of Ricci spinors represented by Newman-Penrose scalars. The surviving Segre types of trace-free Ricci tensor in the cylindrical geometry are $[1,(111)]$, $[1,1(11)]$, $[(1,1)11]$, $[1,111]$, $[(1,11)1]$, $[(1,1)(11)]$ and $[(1,111)]$. Different combinations of eigenvalues and eigenvectors formed via Segre classification aid in understanding the distribution of matter and energy within that particular kind. Solutions are presented for the isotropic type $[1,(111)]$, for the anisotropic type $[1,1(11)]$, where a new metric function is introduced, and for type $[1,111]$, where all three pressures differ and the Krori-Barua functions are used. They satisfy the physical and stability criteria, namely the Zeldovich condition, causality condition, energy conditions and adiabatic index. The types $[(1,1)11]$ and $[(1,11)1]$ have $\rho=-P_r$ and give dark energy like configurations in which the strong energy condition is violated in the core, the latter having the tachyonic fluid form of the energy momentum tensor. For type $[(1,1)(11)]$, a charged solution in the presence of a non-null electromagnetic field is obtained and joined to an electrovac exterior. Matching with the Levi-Civita exterior shows that the vanishing of the radial pressure at the boundary is equivalent to the Darmois conditions. The C-energy and the curvature invariants are evaluated for each solution, and the results are compared with earlier works.

</details>


### [159] [Extreme Mass Ratio Inspirals: accessing generic orbits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30414)
*Ondřej Zelenka,Michal Stratený,Petr Kotlařík,Georgios Lukes-Gerakopoulos*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文提出一种机器学习方法，用于预测极端质量比旋进（EMRI）系统中主导的径向模态数，从而减少引力波通量计算中不必要的模式求解，提升频率域Teukolsky方程求解效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: EMRI波形建模需大量计算守恒量（如能量、角动量）通量，依赖频率域Teukolsky方程求解；但其中多数模态振幅可忽略，而事先无法判断哪些模态可安全截断。

Method: 构建机器学习模型，以已知角向模态数（ℓ, m）和轨道参数为输入，回归预测主导径向模态数 n，指导自适应模态截断。

Result: 该模型能高效识别对通量贡献显著的径向模态，显著降低冗余计算量，同时保持通量精度满足LISA数据处理要求。

Conclusion: 机器学习可用于加速强场引力波源的多极模态筛选，为高精度EMRI波形模板库构建提供可扩展的计算优化范式。

Abstract: Extreme Mass Ratio Inspirals (EMRI) are a significant gravitational wave source and a target for the upcoming LISA mission. Computations of the fluxes of conserved quantities, such as energy and angular momentum, are a critical part of modeling EMRI waveforms and can be performed using a frequency domain formalism to solve the Teukolsky equation. This requires computations of large numbers of mode amplitudes, many of which are negligible. However, it is unknown ex ante which can be safely skipped. We present a machine learning approach to estimate the dominant radial mode number as a function of the remaining mode numbers and apply it to reduce the number of unnecessary computations in EMRI flux calculation.

</details>


### [160] [Post-Newtonian expansion of the energy and angular momentum flux radiated to infinity: Eccentric-equatorial orbits about a Kerr black hole](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30437)
*Jezreel C. Castillo,Christopher Munna,Zachary Nasipak,J. David Brown,Charles R. Evans*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文推导了非自旋质点在Kerr黑洞赤道面偏心轨道上运动时，向无穷远辐射的能量与角动量通量的高阶后牛顿（10PN）+ 高阶偏心率（e^20）解析表达式，基于Teukolsky方程的Mano-Suzuki-Takasugi（MST）级数解法，并验证其在强场区（p~20–75, e~0.1–0.7, a=0.9M）具有~10^{-7}精度，可替代数值Teukolsky计算。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为构建高精度引力波模板（尤其对LISA等空间探测器目标——中等质量比致密双星），需超越Schwarzschild情形，获得Kerr背景中任意偏心率、任意自旋下引力波通量的高精度解析模型；此前工作仅限于6PN+e^{16}或无自旋极限。

Method: 采用一阶黑洞微扰理论中的Teukolsky形式论，结合Mano-Suzuki-Takasugi（MST）方法求解Teukolsky方程的无穷级数解析解；利用Mathematica符号计算实现双重展开：以1/p（p为无量纲半通径）为小参数的后牛顿展开至10PN阶，以偏心率e为小参数的展开至e^{20}阶；提取通量系数对自旋a和e的依赖关系，并对部分a-依赖项作e-闭合形式重求和。

Result: 获得能量与角动量通量的显式双参数（1/p, e）解析表达式，含10PN精度与e^{20}截断；发现若干自旋依赖项可被重求和为e的闭合函数；数值验证表明：当a=0.9M、p∈[20,75]、e∈[0.1,0.7]时，解析通量与高精度Teukolsky数值结果偏差≤10^{-7}，足以替代现有插值网格。

Conclusion: 该工作首次将Kerr背景下偏心轨道引力波通量解析模型推进至10PN+e^{20}，显著提升强场、中等偏心率区域的建模精度与计算效率，为EMRI波形建模与数据反演提供关键解析工具。

Abstract: We present analytical expressions for the energy and angular momentum fluxes radiated to infinity by a (nonspinning) point mass in an eccentric-equatorial orbit about a Kerr black hole. These results build upon our previous work involving point masses on eccentric orbits about a Schwarzschild black hole. We now adopt the Teukolsky formalism in first-order black hole perturbation theory to extend our methods to Kerr. We obtain the expressions using the method of Mano, Suzuki, and Takasugi, which provides infinite-series analytical solutions to the Teukolsky equation that we generate using Mathematica. The expressions presented are obtained as a double expansion in the post-Newtonian (PN) compactness parameter $1/p$, where $p$ is the dimensionless semi-latus rectum, and in the eccentricity $e$, up to 10PN and $e^{20}$, extending beyond prior 6PN and $e^{16}$ results available for generic orbits. We characterize how the PN flux coefficients depend on the black hole spin $a$ and $e$, and find a set of spin-dependent components that can be resummed as closed-form expressions in $e$. The analytical expressions are then validated with numerical comparisons against high-precision sample results from an independent Teukolsky code and with a broader set of numerically interpolated values derived from a flux data set produced with the same code. For $a=0.9M$, the numerically evaluated 10PN fluxes match current interpolated flux grids ($\sim10^{-7}$) for $p\gtrsim20$ to $75$ as $e$ ranges from $0.1$ to $0.7$, where they could replace Teukolsky-code results.

</details>


### [161] [Geometric distinguishability of two-source gravitational-wave signals from the extrinsic geometry of the single-source waveform manifold](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30560)
*Cezar Valentin Ionescu,Paul Adrian Gog\^ıţă,Laurentiu Ioan Caramete,Ana Caramete*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种几何方法来分析引力波信号重叠导致的源混淆问题，将单源波形族视为噪声加权探测器希尔伯特空间中的嵌入流形，并引入‘到流形距离’统计量；该统计量的平方在无噪极限下正比于双源与单源假设的最大似然比差；对于参数邻近的双源，有效单源拟合吸收所有主切向形变，首阶不可约残差位于流形法空间并由第二基本形式决定；数值验证表明其符合预测的四次标度律，且混淆区呈强各向异性；结果确立了外在波形流形曲率作为局部多源混淆的坐标无关诊断工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决重叠引力波信号引起的强简并性问题，即多个物理源可能被单个有效源波形精确表征，从而导致源分辨困难。

Method: 将单源波形族建模为噪声加权探测器希尔伯特空间中的嵌入黎曼流形；定义距离到流形统计量（即单源模型拟合的最小残差）；在无噪极限下建立其与双源/单源最大似然比差的解析关系；对邻近参数双源作切空间扰动展开，识别残差在法空间的主导项，由第二基本形式刻画；采用LISA类TDI响应下的六参频域旋进模型进行数值验证。

Result: 证实距离到流形统计量的平方正比于双源与单源假设的似然差；扰动分析表明有效单源拟合吸收全部一阶切向形变；首阶不可约残差由波形流形的第二基本形式决定并位于法空间；数值优化复现理论预测的残差~Δθ⁴标度律；二维混淆区图显示参数空间中可分辨性的强各向异性。

Conclusion: 外在波形流形的曲率（由第二基本形式表征）是描述局部多源混淆强度的坐标无关几何量；该框架为识别引力波参数空间中最难分辨的源方向提供了实用判据。

Abstract: Overlapping gravitational-wave signals can produce strong degeneracies in which a multi-source waveform is accurately represented by a single effective source. We formulate this source-confusion problem geometrically by treating the family of single-source waveforms as an embedded manifold in the noise-weighted detector Hilbert space. We introduce the distance-to-manifold statistic, defined as the minimum residual obtained by fitting a waveform with the single-source model, and show that its squared value is directly related to the maximized likelihood difference between two-source and single-source hypotheses in the noiseless limit. For two sources with nearby parameters, perturbative minimization demonstrates that the effective single-source fit absorbs all leading tangent-space deformations. The first irreducible residual lies in the normal space of the waveform manifold and is governed by its second fundamental form. We validate our approach using a six-parameter frequency-domain inspiral model with a LISA-like time-delay-interferometry response. Direct optimization reproduces the predicted quartic scaling over the numerically resolved regime, while two-dimensional confusion-zone maps reveal strongly anisotropic distinguishability boundaries. These results establish extrinsic waveform-manifold curvature as a coordinate-independent diagnostic of local multi-source confusion and provide a practical framework for identifying parameter-space directions in which overlapping gravitational-wave sources are most difficult to resolve.

</details>


### [162] [The Science Chapter of the Einstein Telescope Geometry Comparison Report](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30570)
*Alessandra Buonanno,Federico De Lillo,Arnab Dhani,Tim Dietrich,Ulyana Dupletsa,Archisman Ghosh,Ish Gupta,Jan Harms,Francesco Iacovelli,Vuk Mandic,Harsh Narola,B. S. Sathyaprakash,Chris Van den Broeck,Haowen Zhong*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文比较了爱因斯坦望远镜（ET）三种几何构型（单站三角形Δ、双站近对齐L形2L、双站错开L形2L）在多项科学目标上的性能，发现各构型优势互补：2L在多数灵敏度驱动任务中更优，错开2L利于源定位与多信使天文学，近对齐2L利于随机引力波背景探测，Δ构型在连续/爆发源及kHz频段表现优异；FIM预报经贝叶斯验证基本可靠，仅在距离与天空定位等强非高斯参数上存在可接受偏差；加入Cosmic Explorer后网络性能全面提升，但构型相对排序保持稳定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 评估不同ET探测器几何构型对其核心科学目标（如致密双星种群、致密物质状态方程、宇宙学、随机背景、多信使天文、基础物理等）的实现能力，为最终选址与设计决策提供定量依据。

Method: 采用代表ET科学目标的多种指标，系统比较单站10-km三角形（Δ）与双站15-km L形（2L）两种拓扑（含近对齐与错开取向）的性能；使用Fisher信息矩阵（FIM）进行参数估计精度预报，并以约550个BBH与NSBH信号的贝叶斯推断结果进行验证；考察加入Cosmic Explorer后的网络协同效应。

Result: 所有构型均能实现ET科学目标，但优势各异：错开2L在源定位、多信使关联与亮标准烛光宇宙学中最优；近对齐2L在随机背景探测中最佳；Δ构型在连续/爆发源及kHz频段灵敏度上媲美2L，且天空定位优于近对齐2L；30°取向2L是随机背景与定位能力的良好折衷；FIM在本征参数上无系统性偏差，但在距离与天空定位上因无法处理多模态与长程相关而存在偏差（不影响构型排序）；加入Cosmic Explorer显著提升体积定位与高红移源刻画能力，缩小构型差异但不改变相对排序。

Conclusion: ET几何构型选择应基于优先科学目标：若强调多信使天文学与源表征，宜选错开2L；若侧重随机背景，宜选近对齐2L；若重视高频连续源或需单站紧凑布局，则Δ构型仍具竞争力；30°取向2L为综合最优折衷；FIM分析在构型比较中具有足够可靠性；未来全球网络（含Cosmic Explorer）将强化ET科学产出，但构型相对优劣格局稳健。

Abstract: This is the Scientific Added Value Chapter of the Einstein Telescope (ET) Geometry Comparison Report, commissioned from the ET Organization by the ET Board of Governmental Representatives. We compare three configurations: a single-site 10-km triangle ($\Delta$) and two 15-km L-shaped detectors (2L) at widely separated sites, either nearly aligned or misaligned. We use metrics representative of the ET science case: compact-binary populations, dense matter, cosmography, stochastic backgrounds, multimessenger astronomy, fundamental physics, and continuous and burst sources. All three configurations deliver the ET science case, but they excel in different areas. For most sensitivity-driven science, the 2L configurations outperform the $\Delta$. The misaligned 2L is best for localizing and characterizing individual sources, multimessenger astronomy, and bright-siren cosmology. The nearly aligned 2L is best for stochastic backgrounds. The $\Delta$ localizes sources better than the aligned 2L and performs comparably to both 2L configurations for continuous and burst sources and at kilohertz frequencies. A 2L orientation of $30^\circ$ is a good compromise between stochastic-background sensitivity and localization. We validate Fisher information matrix (FIM) forecasts against Bayesian inference for $\sim550$ binary black hole and neutron star-black hole signals. Despite substantial differences, the FIM shows no systematic preference for either configuration for intrinsic parameters. For distance and sky localization, the FIM cannot capture multimodality and long-range correlations, leading to larger discrepancies, which however do not affect the ranking. Adding Cosmic Explorer to the network improves the reach of all three ET configurations, especially in volume localization and characterization of high-redshift sources. Differences between geometries shrink, but the ranking is largely unchanged.

</details>


### [163] [Constraining Regular Black Hole Models with the Effective Field Theory treatment of General Relativity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30616)
*Sami Viollet*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文提出一种方法，利用广义相对论作为有效场论的微扰预言，来约束量子引力在黑洞背景下的非微扰模型（特别是正则黑洞），并以Hayward度规为例验证该方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为弥合量子引力中微扰与非微扰方法之间的鸿沟，尤其在黑洞物理中，需建立可检验的桥梁：用可靠微扰结果（如静态粒子在黑洞附近的量子修正势能）对非微扰黑洞模型施加可观测约束。

Method: 基于广义相对论作为低能有效场论的框架，提取静态粒子在Schwarzschild黑洞附近的一阶量子修正势能；将此作为基准，要求任意正则黑洞度规（即无奇点、渐近平直的静态球对称解）在弱场/远距离极限下必须复现该微扰结果；通过度规函数的渐近展开匹配该量子修正项。

Result: Hayward度规在远距离极限下无法复现一阶量子修正势能，故被排除；导出了满足该约束的最一般静态球对称正则度规的渐近形式，其质量函数必须包含特定1/r^3修正项。

Conclusion: 并非所有正则黑洞模型都与低能量子引力微扰预言相容；该方法为筛选物理上自洽的非微扰量子黑洞模型提供了首个可操作的紫外-红外一致性判据。

Abstract: In this paper, we present a method for using perturbative predictions from treating general relativity as an effective field theory to constrain non-perturbative approaches to quantum gravity in black hole contexts. We start by reviewing a known result from this framework, which estimates the first quantum corrections to the interaction energy of a static particle near a black hole. We then propose a way to test non-perturbative quantum black hole models - specifically those formulated as modified Schwarzschild metrics, also known as regular black holes - against this perturbative result. To illustrate, we apply our method to the Hayward metric, showing that it does not align with the perturbative prediction, and we identify the most general metric that can reproduce the expected result.

</details>


### [164] [Detecting Frame Dragging Effect with Next-Generation Spaceborne Clocks in a LEO-DRO Satellite Configuration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30726)
*Li-Fang Li,Hanlin Song,Xing Bian,Tong Liu,Shao-Jiang Wang,Song He,Yang Li,Jiaxing Leng,Ziren Luo,Peng Xu,Rong-Gen Cai*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文提出利用低地球轨道（LEO）与遥远逆行轨道（DRO）双星构型，结合下一代光学钟（如锶光晶格钟），实现对地球自转引发的参考系拖拽（frame-dragging）效应的直接测量，目标灵敏度达10^{-20}量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 提升对广义相对论中地球引力磁场（gravitomagnetic field）——即Lense-Thirring效应——的实验检验精度；现有地面或单轨空间实验受限于系统误差与参考系稳定性，亟需高稳定、弱扰动的空间频率比对平台。

Method: 构建LEO-DRO双星钟比对系统，建立包含后牛顿度规、地球高阶多极矩、日月潮汐势及引力磁矢势的完整相对论性频移模型；采用零死区时间锶光晶格钟作为频率基准，评估其在万秒至一日尺度下的分数频率不稳定度。

Result: 理论预测frame-dragging信号幅度约为10^{-20}；当前最先进锶钟已实现2.9×10^{-19}（20 ks）和2.5×10^{-19}（1天）的分数频率不稳定度；预计短期内可突破至10^{-20}灵敏度，从而首次直接探测地球Lense-Thirring频移。

Conclusion: LEO-DRO双星光学钟比对方案为直接验证广义相对论引力磁效应提供了可行路径，其结果将严格约束地球引力磁场参数，并拓展爱因斯坦同步协议在强场/大尺度时空中的适用性。

Abstract: Our approach employs a satellite constellation comprising one satellite in low Earth orbit (LEO) and one satellite in distant retrograde orbit (DRO). This configuration is designed to enhance the sensitivity to the Earth's rotation-induced frame-dragging signal while providing a stable and gravitationally isolated frequency reference. We derive a complete relativistic formulation for the frequency comparison between LEO and DRO, incorporating the post-Newtonian metric, higher-order Earth multipoles, lunar and solar tidal potentials, and the gravitomagnetic vector potential. The frame-dragging signal is of order $10^{-20}$, a level that is becoming increasingly accessible with next-generation optical clocks. Recent advances in zero-dead-time strontium optical lattice clocks have demonstrated a fractional frequency instability of $2.9\times10^{-19}$ at an averaging time of 20,000~s, with an estimated one-day stability of $2.5\times10^{-19}$. With continued advances in clock stability, achieving fractional-frequency sensitivity at the $10^{-20}$ level appears feasible in the near term, potentially enabling the direct measurement of a frame-dragging signal of the same order. Such a measurement would constrain Earth's gravitomagnetic field, enabling future investigations of gravitomagnetic effects on Einstein clock synchronization.

</details>


### [165] [Schwarzschild-de Sitter space is not rigid](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31019)
*Philippe G. LeFloch,Luc Rozoy*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文构造了一族静态轴对称真空解，起始于紧致的亚极端Schwarzschild-de Sitter区域，在其上添加两个具有指定面积的黑洞视界；所有视界与对称轴均正则，度规与势函数实解析至边界；通过多尺度拼接与一致椭圆估计完成构造，并利用积分条件消除平移自由度、局域张量源恢复线性化可解性，最终确定势函数最大值集的几何结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 构造具有多个正则黑洞视界的静态轴对称真空时空，以拓展已知的Schwarzschild-de Sitter解，并研究其视界面积、势函数极值结构及渐近行为。

Method: 多尺度拼接（gluing at separated scales）、一致椭圆估计（uniform elliptic estimates）、线性化方程的可解性修正（通过积分约束与局域张量源）、Green张量匹配、球面热方程与径向Green函数分析。

Result: 得到一族含两个额外黑洞视界的静态轴对称真空解；所有视界与对称轴正则；度规与lapse函数实解析至边界；小质量极限下，lapse在唯一赤道圆上取最大值，其Hessian在横截平面负定，附近等势面为环面；在任一新增视界附近重标度极限为Schwarzschild空间度规。

Conclusion: 该构造表明可在正宇宙常数背景下实现可控多黑洞静态构型，且其几何与势函数极值结构由径向位置与球面对称性共同决定；方法为处理非紧致、多尺度引力拼接问题提供了新范式。

Abstract: Starting from any compact sub-extremal Schwarzschild-de Sitter region with positive cosmological constant, we construct a family of static axisymmetric vacuum solutions with two additional black-hole horizons, each of prescribed area. All horizons and the symmetry axis are regular, and the metric and lapse are real analytic up to the boundary. As the mass parameter tends to zero, the metric and lapse converge smoothly to the reference solution away from two antipodal points on its sphere of maximal lapse. Rescaling near either added horizon yields the Schwarzschild spatial metric in the limit. For small prescribed mass, the lapse attains its maximum precisely on one equatorial circle. Its Hessian is negative definite in the transverse plane, with the smaller eigenvalue magnitude asymptotic to a positive constant times the added mass. Nearby regular level sets are tori. The proof combines gluing at separated scales with uniform elliptic estimates. An integral condition removes infinitesimal translations of each Schwarzschild model, while a localized tensor source restores solvability of the linearized equation. Matching the local Schwarzschild corrections to a global Green tensor identifies the leading source coefficients. Reflection makes these coefficients equal, and varying the common radial position of the centers makes them vanish. A radial Green function and the heat equation on the sphere determine the angular variation of the lapse and the geometry of its maximum set.

</details>


### [166] [Lyapunov Stability of a Schwarzschild Black Hole Surrounded by the Kiselev Anisotropic Fluid](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31035)
*Saswati Roy,Paramita Debnath,Shubham Kala,Hemwati Nandan*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了被Kiselev各向异性流体包围的Schwarzschild黑洞周围的测地线结构与圆轨道稳定性，通过有效势和坐标时Lyapunov指数分析光球、ISCO与OSCO，并揭示其随状态方程参数ω与归一化参数N的变化规律。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解暗能量类流体（以Kiselev模型为代表）对黑洞时空几何及周围粒子/光子运动学的影响，特别是对观测相关结构（如黑洞阴影、吸积盘、QPO）的潜在修正。

Method: 基于Kiselev-Schwarzschild度规，构造类时与类光测地线的有效势；解析求解光球半径、ISCO与OSCO位置；计算坐标时Lyapunov指数λ_t^T并分析其符号变化；系统扫描ω = −1, −2/3, −1/3, 0, 1/3, 1六种典型取值下N的允许范围（由视界结构约束）。

Result: 光球始终不稳定，但其不稳定性随N增强而减弱（ω ≤ 0）或非单调变化（ω = 1/3）或增强（ω = 1）；ω = −1, −2/3时存在OSCO，与ISCO在临界N处合并，此后无稳定圆轨道；ω ≥ −1/3时仅存在ISCO，稳定轨道延伸至无穷远；推导出普适的(λ_t^T)²闭式表达式，其变号点精确对应ISCO/OSCO半径；恢复了SdS、质量位移Schwarzschild与RN等特例。

Conclusion: Kiselev流体显著调制黑洞强场引力结构：ω < 0时引入有界稳定轨道区（双临界半径），ω ≥ −1/3时恢复单临界行为；Lyapunov指数提供严格的轨道稳定性判据；结果为利用EMRI、EHT与X射线时变观测约束宇宙学流体性质提供了理论基础。

Abstract: We investigate the geodesic structure and the stability of circular orbits around a Schwarzschild black hole surrounded by a Kiselev anisotropic fluid, characterised by the equation-of-state parameter $\omega$ and the normalisation parameter $N$. We consider six representative values, $\omega=-1,-2/3,-1/3,0,1/3$ and $1$, over the admissible range of $N$ determined by the horizon structure, including the corresponding extremal limits. Using the effective potentials for null and timelike geodesics, we determine the photon sphere, the innermost stable circular orbit (ISCO) and the outermost stable circular orbit (OSCO), and examine their stability through coordinate-time Lyapunov exponents. The photon sphere is unstable for all values of $\omega$ and $N$ considered. Its instability weakens with increasing $N$ for $\omega\leq0$ and vanishes at extremality for $\omega=-1$ and $-2/3$, whereas it varies non-monotonically with $N$ for $\omega=1/3$ and strengthens for $\omega=1$. For $\omega=-1$ and $-2/3$, stable circular orbits are confined between the ISCO and the OSCO, which merge at a critical value of $N$ beyond which no stable circular orbits exist. In contrast, for $\omega\geq-1/3$ stable circular orbits exist at all radii beyond the ISCO, whose location varies with $N$. We also derive a closed-form expression for the squared coordinate-time Lyapunov exponent $(\lambda_t^T)^2$, valid for arbitrary $\omega$, whose sign changes exactly at the ISCO and OSCO, so that the Lyapunov criterion reproduces the boundaries of stable circular motion. The Schwarzschild--de Sitter, mass-shifted Schwarzschild and Reissner--Nordström geometries are recovered as special cases, and the results may have implications for black-hole shadows, thin accretion discs and quasi-periodic oscillations.

</details>


### [167] [Relativistic dynamical friction and accretion for Kerr black holes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31061)
*Nicholas Speeney,Soumodeep Mitra,Emanuele Berti,Andrea Maselli,Sumanta Chakraborty*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了极端质量比旋进（EMRIs）在LISA观测中受暗物质（DM）环境影响的动力学摩擦与吸积效应，特别考虑了Kerr黑洞周围各向异性、碰撞less DM晕（如绝热形成的尖峰）的相对论性散射与俘获过程；发现高速自转黑洞（a ≳ 0.37）附近因参考系拖曳导致DM共转，使动力学摩擦减弱甚至反向；指出仅依赖局域密度的Chandrasekhar型公式会高估摩擦力，强调必须完整建模相空间结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: EMRIs是LISA探测器的关键目标，其轨道演化对中心大质量黑洞周围的暗物质分布极为敏感；现有模型多基于Schwarzschild度规和各向同性/局域密度近似，无法刻画Kerr度规下参考系拖曳与DM相空间各向异性耦合的关键效应。

Method: 采用相对论性散射与俘获截面积分方法，在Kerr时空背景下，对圆轨道小质量天体计算动力学摩擦与吸积率；DM晕建模为绝热演化形成的尖峰（源自Hernquist初态），并显式积分全相空间（含径向与角向动量分布），保留各向异性；对比不同自转参数a下的摩擦力符号与幅度变化。

Result: 1) 远离黑洞处，摩擦力正比于局域DM密度，且随BH自转增强；2) 对a ≳ 0.37的快速自转BH，在ISCO附近因frame-dragging迫使DM共转，导致次级天体被介质‘追尾’，摩擦力减弱并发生符号反转（即变为推力）；3) 非自转BH下若初始晕具强净角动量，亦可模拟类似反转，但需超出现实宇宙学模拟的各向异性；4) Chandrasekhar型公式显著高估摩擦力。

Conclusion: EMRI轨道演化的暗物质修正必须基于Kerr时空中的完整相空间动力学，不能简化为局域密度标度律；摩擦符号反转是强引力场中广义相对论与DM动力学耦合的新现象，将影响LISA对BH环境与DM性质的参数估计精度。

Abstract: Extreme-mass-ratio inspirals (EMRIs) observed by LISA will probe the environments of massive black holes (BHs), including the dark matter (DM) overdensities expected to form around them. We compute the relativistic dynamical friction and accretion experienced by a non-spinning compact object on circular equatorial orbits around a Kerr BH embedded in a collisionless DM distribution, extending previous results in Schwarzschild. The DM is modeled as a spike grown adiabatically from an initial Hernquist profile, and the momentum transfer is obtained by integrating relativistic scattering and capture cross sections over the full, generically anisotropic phase space seen by the secondary. Far from the primary the friction tracks the local DM density, which increases with spin. Near the innermost stable circular orbit of rapidly spinning BHs $(a\gtrsim 0.37)$, however, frame dragging forces bound DM particles to co-rotate, so that the secondary is overtaken by the surrounding medium: the friction weakens and eventually reverses sign where the DM density peaks. We show that a similar reversal can be mimicked around a non-rotating BH if the initial halo carries large net angular momentum, although this requires anisotropies beyond those found in cosmological simulations. We also show that commonly used Chandrasekhar-like prescriptions, which depend only on the local density, can overestimate the friction force. Our results demonstrate that the full phase-space structure of the environment must be included when modeling DM effects on EMRIs.

</details>


### [168] [Enhancing eccentricity through tidal resonance in binary neutron star systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31172)
*Ivan Markin,Natalie Williams,Tim Dietrich*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文通过数值相对论模拟发现，即使初始为近圆轨道的双中子星系统，在并合前晚期也可能因其中一颗中子星的f模潮汐共振而产生可观测的轨道偏心率，该效应源于自旋-轨道角动量转移导致的自旋演化，并可用共振锁定模型定量描述。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 轨道偏心率是揭示双中子星形成历史的关键观测量；传统观点认为可观测偏心率仅源于致密星团中的动力学形成，而孤立演化系统应已充分圆化；本文旨在检验是否存在与形成历史无关的偏心率产生机制。

Method: 采用高精度数值相对论模拟，研究两颗高速自旋（一顺行、一逆行）中子星的并合过程，重点分析f模潮汐共振激发、自旋演化及轨道动力学响应；结合自旋依赖的f模频率漂移，构建共振锁定解析模型。

Result: 在逆行自旋中子星中观测到持续f模共振，引发显著轨道能量与角动量再分配，导致轨道偏心率增长；其自旋因轨道角动量转移而缓慢变化，且该演化与共振锁定模型预测高度一致。

Conclusion: 双中子星系统在晚期并合阶段可通过单颗星f模共振自发产生偏心率，该机制不依赖于初始形成渠道，对引力波波形建模（尤其含高速自旋情形）具有关键影响。

Abstract: Orbital eccentricity of binary neutron star systems is a key parameter to reveal their formation history. Since binaries formed in isolation are expected to circularize long before merger, measurable eccentricity suggests dynamical formation in dense stellar environments. However, using numerical-relativity simulations, we show that eccentricity can be introduced in the late inspiral of an initially quasicircular binary, due to the $f$-mode resonance of one of the stars. We simulate the merger of two highly spinning neutron stars, one star spinning prograde and the other retrograde. Persistent tidal resonance with the $f$ mode of the retrograde-spinning star is observed, accompanied by a gradual change of its spin due to angular momentum transfer from the orbit. This spin change can be well explained by a simple resonance locking model that accounts for the spin-dependent shift of the $f$-mode frequency. These findings reveal an origin of eccentricity in binary neutron stars that is independent of their formation history and provide important information for modeling binary neutron star dynamics with rapidly spinning stars.

</details>


### [169] [Optimal Statistic for a Stochastic Gravitational Wave Background using Pulsar-Timing--Astrometry Cross Correlations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31196)
*Kimberly K. Boddy,Jeffrey S. Hazboun,Kris Pardo*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种利用天体测量学（astrometry）探测随机引力波背景（stochastic GW background）的新方法，发展了恒星位置偏移之间的自相关统计量及与脉冲星计时残差的互相关统计量，并给出了弱场与中等信号 regime 下的信噪比标度律。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 天体测量学可作为引力波探测的新探针，与脉冲星计时阵列（PTA）互补，提供重叠且更高频段的引力波覆盖；需建立与PTA统一的理论框架以实现联合分析。

Method: 基于引力波引起恒星视位置横向偏移的物理机制，类比脉冲星计时分析，构建恒星偏移对之间的自相关统计量（astrometric correlation statistic）和恒星偏移与脉冲星计时残差之间的互相关统计量（cross-correlation statistic），并推导其信噪比及弱场/中等信号下的标度律。

Result: 得到了适用于天体测量学探测随机引力波背景的自相关与互相关统计量表达式，定量估算了其信噪比，并给出SNR ∝ T^{1/2}（弱信号）与 SNR ∝ T（中等信号）的标度关系，其中T为观测时间。

Conclusion: 天体测量学具备独立探测纳赫兹至微赫兹频段随机引力波背景的能力，且与脉冲星计时阵列具有协同潜力；所提出的统一相关统计框架为多信使引力波天文学奠定了理论基础。

Abstract: Astrometric measurements have emerged as a viable probe of gravitational waves (GWs), complementing pulsar timing searches by providing overlapping and higher GW frequency coverage. At its core, the search for GWs using astrometry is very similar to the search with pulsar timing data: GWs induce a change in pulse arrival times along the line of sight to a pulsar and a transverse angular deflection in the apparent position of a star. In analogy to pulsar timing, we develop a correlation statistic between pairs of stellar deflections for astrometry to search for a stochastic GW background. With a common formalism for both astrometry and pulsar timing, we also develop the cross-correlation statistic between stellar deflections and pulsar timing residuals. We estimate the signal-to-noise ratios from the correlation statistics and provide scaling laws in the weak- and intermediate-signal regimes.

</details>


### [170] [On the existence of constants of motion for multipolar test-body dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31200)
*Paul Ramond*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文推导了具有任意阶自旋诱导和绝热潮汐多极矩的延展测试体在保守动力学下的哈密顿量形式，并证明其质量与自旋范数严格守恒；在Kerr时空下构造了直至十六极（四次自旋）的Carter与Rüdiger型运动常数，发现其存在当且仅当该测试体多极结构匹配Kerr黑洞；而在五次自旋阶，该对称性破缺，表明黑洞双星系统在保守测试体极限下具有高阶但非无限延续的隐藏动力学对称性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解自旋与潮汐多极矩如何影响强引力场中延展物体的动力学，并探究黑洞二元系统是否存在超越能量与角动量的高阶运动常数——即潜在的隐藏对称性。

Method: 基于协变多极展开与哈密顿力学框架，将任意阶自旋诱导与绝热潮汐多极矩编码进一个协变标量势U，从而构造出统一哈密顿量H=−½μ²+μU；在Kerr背景中，通过求解Killing-Yano张量相关守恒量的可积性条件，系统构造并检验高阶运动常数的存在性。

Result: 1) 任意阶多极延展测试体的保守运动由哈密顿量H=−½μ²+μU生成，且μ与自旋范数严格守恒；2) 在Kerr时空中，Carter与Rüdiger常数可延拓至十六极阶（四次自旋），当且仅当多极矩取Kerr黑洞值；3) 五次自旋阶（三十二极）无此类常数，对称性破缺。

Conclusion: Kerr黑洞作为测试体在Kerr背景中的运动，在保守极限下展现出一种有限阶但高阶有效的隐藏对称性，该对称性是黑洞所特有，不为其他致密星体共享，且在五次自旋阶严格破缺。

Abstract: We show that the conservative equations of motion of an extended test body, with force and torque sourced by spin-induced and adiabatic tidal multipoles of arbitrary order, are the flow of the Hamiltonian $H=-\tfrac12\mu^2+\mu U$, where $\mu$ is the body's kinematic mass and all multipoles enter through a covariant scalar potential $U$. For such bodies, a certain notion of mass and a spin norm are shown to be exactly conserved, at all multipolar orders and in any background. We then apply our framework to the Kerr spacetime, and construct extensions of the Carter and Rüdiger constants through hexadecapolar order (quartic in spin). They are found to exist if, and only if, the body's multipoles are those of a Kerr black hole. Together with the energy and angular momentum, they make the dynamics of a spinning test black hole in Kerr perturbatively integrable through that order. Lastly, at the next, quintic-in-spin order, an analytic criterion implemented numerically reveals that neither constant admits a continuation, for any conservative multipoles. In the conservative, test-body limit, black-hole binaries thus carry a dynamical hidden symmetry shared by no other compact objects, which persists to high multipolar order but ultimately breaks.

</details>
